A Fiery Apparition is the third stage of the adventure "To End An Ice Age". This stage is unlocked by opening the coffin that unlocks Bianca Ramba in the preceding stage, Ice Cream.
If the player opens the coffin in this stage, they can unlock Cosmo Pavone, as well as the succeeding stage, A Pure Heart.
## Description
No. Ramba spits fiery insults at Mortaccio, her broken heart still burning with rage - but the skeleton ignores her, too busy crying (figuratively) over spilt (and now frozen) milk. But something catches She-Moon's ear in Ramba's scatching rant: the legend of the firebird.

A Pure Heart is the fourth stage of the adventure "To End An Ice Age". This stage is unlocked by opening the coffin that unlocks Cosmo Pavone in the preceding stage, A Fiery Apparition.
If the player opens the coffin in this stage, they can unlock Christine Davain, as well as the succeeding stage, The Rebirth, The Return.
## Description
The firebird, Cosmo Pavone, is currently ignoring our heroes and eating toasted seeds. Clearly none of them have truly good intentions, as Cosmo will only aid those of pure heart. She-Moon searches for a real hero, a guardian who will fight for love and justice.

Abyss Foscari is the second stage in the Tides of the Foscari DLC for Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked after breaking the green crystal (Seal of the Lake) in Lake Foscari with Keitha Muort and her evolved base weapon, Millionaire.
## Description
Sealed by Luminaire in ancient times to contain the darkness within, these underground ruins are now accessible, but so is the outside world to the evil from the depths.
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this stage can be unlocked by defeating the Je-Ne-Viv in normal mode.
Hyper mode in Abyss Foscari has a +100% Gold and +10% Luck bonus.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
- Abyss Foscari map with full item spawn locations.The map has a red crystal (Seal of the Abyss) that has to be destroyed by  Maruto Cuts and the  Legionnaire, his evolved base weapon. It opens up the section of the map that is covered in darkness.
- After destroying the red crystal, a blue crystal (Seal of the Banished) is unlocked as the darkness recedes. It has to be destroyed by  Eleanor Uziron and the  SpellStrom, her united base weapon. Destroying the blue crystal unlocks the Je-Ne-Viv boss fight.
## Stage items
-  Armor: south of starting area in the upper path.
-  Empty Tome: 10%, 10%, 10%, 10% chance for more Empty Tome to spawn. West of starting area at the top of the hidden room. The entrance to the room is slightly south of the Empty Tome.
-  Attractorb: 20%, 20%, 20%, 20%, 20% chance for more Attractorb to spawn. West of the starting area along the right wall leading towards Je-Ne-Viv.
-  Crown: 20%, 20%, 20%, 20% chance for more Crown to spawn. Far south of the starting area.
-  Stone Mask: 20%, 20%, 20% chance for more Stone Mask to spawn. West of the starting area along the left wall leading towards Je-Ne-Viv.
-  Skull O'Maniac: 20%, 20%, 20%, 20% chance for more Skull O'Maniac to spawn. Southwest of the starting area in the upper path before the Arcana treasure chest.
-  Torrona's Box: Far southwest of the starting area at the bottom of the hidden room. The entrance to the room is lightly south of the Empty Tome.
-  Academy Badge
- Available after Maruto has broken the Seal of the Abyss southwest of the starting area in the lower path and is located there.
-  Prismatic Missile: Southwest of the starting area in the lower path.
-  Shadow Servant
- Available after Eleanor has broken the Seal of the Banished and is located near Je-Ni-Viv west of the starting area.
-  Arcana Treasure Chest: southwest of the starting area in the middle of the upper path.
The 4 hidden stage items are also present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.
-  Silver Ring: southwest of starting area.
-  Metaglio Right: southwest of starting area.
-  Gold Ring: southwest of starting area.
-  Metaglio Left: southwest of starting area.
- They are positioned surrounding an Arcana Treasure Chest in the middle of the upper path, guarding it with the Atlanteans.
## Pickups
- 4x  Vacuum:
- Two are near the starting area.
- One is south of the starting area near the Crown spawn location.
- One is southwest of the starting area near Prismatic Missile.

## Traps
Stepping on one of the small black circles on the ground triggers an unavoidable map event, chosen at random between 11 predetermined options. The traps have a 20 second cooldown before they can be triggered again.
| Trap events in Abyss Foscari
| Event | Unit | Amount | Duration (seconds) | Range | Notes
| Pile Assault | Lost Head | 40 | 5.0 | 0.7 | The event summons 40 Lost Heads in an elliptic shape around the player. They cannot move, fire every 2 seconds, have a max health of 25.
| Pile Assault | Lost Head 2 | 30 | 5.0 | 0.7 | The event summons Lost Head 2s, which fire every 1.5 seconds and have a max health of 150.
| Pile Assault | Lost Head 2 | 20 | 5.0 | 0.7 | -
| Jellyfish Wall | Rellyfish | 80 | 30 | 0.9 | The event summons Rellyfishes in an elliptic shape around the player.
| Jellyfish Wall | Rellyfish | 40 | 30 | 0.9 | -
| Swarm | Swarm Rotting Ghoul | 40 | 10 | 0.9 | The event spawns a group of Swarm Rotting Ghouls, which sweep in a straight line across the map and can push other enemies along them.
| Swarm | Swarm Rotting Ghoul | 20 | 10 | 0.9 | -

| Swarm | Burning Skull | 50 | 20 | 0.9 | This event is the same as above, appearing a second time in the list of events.
## Trivia
- Six Attractorbs can spawn on Abyss Foscari, five of which are at 20%, but since you can only have five levels, the sixth is removed once Attractorb is fully leveled.
- By default, enemies on this stage exhibit a stop-and-go movement behavior in reference to Crypt of the NecroDancer. After defeating Je-Ne-Viv, there will be a blue hourglass button just north of the starting area that can be used to disable this feature for the remainder of the current run.
- Abyss Foscari and Red Moon Manor are currently the only stage where a Torrona's Box can spawn in.
- However, because Arma Dios can spawn in Ode to Castlevania, it is possible to obtain the item there as well.
=====
# Adventures
The Adventures menu with only base game adventures listed.
Adventures are a game mode introduced in the 1.8 update. They are unlocked after collecting the  Atlas Gate in Boss Rash for base-game adventures, being A Garlic Paradise, World of Light and Dark, and To End an Ice Age. DLC Adventures, such as Tides of the Foscari, can be unlocked much earlier, being unlocked when the player unlocks the Dairy Plant Stage.
Each adventure features a linear progression of stages and unlocks with a predetermined roster of characters. While the game's description of them says that no progress carries over, achievements can be completed and relics can be used and collected within them, even if their prerequisite cannot be accessed.
Golden Eggs cannot be activated in any adventure, however they can be gained by purchasing them or defeating certain enemies, and will apply to the character that received them outside of the adventure. 
## Ascension
Once all unlocks of an Adventure are complete, it can be ascended to reset the progress and gain an ascension point. Ascension points can be spent on Luck, Growth, Gold, or Curse, with each point granting +25%.
## List of Adventures
| Image | Adventure
| | A Garlic Paradise
| | World of Light and Dark
| | To End an Ice Age
| | Legacy of the Moonspell
| | Emergency Meeting
| | Operation Guns
| | Tides of the Foscari

Ante Chamber is a stage in the eponymous DLC of Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by obtaining the  Joker Disk, which can be bought from  Xanthia in Moongolow for 50,000.
## Description
Fearsome fools flock to this extradimensional card carnival, where survival is a high-stakes game. The beastly buffoons that prowl this floating funhouse aren’t bluffing, so play your cards right or the joke will be on you.
## Overview
This stage is based on the interface of Balatro. The dark gray panels are the walkable areas while the green background is the "sky".
There are 3 dark gray panels that the player can walk on:
- The middle panel, which is the panel that the player spawns on, is vertically infinite, and is connected to the two other panels through two bridges made out of black cards. This panel has a "SHOP" label, 2 red buttons, 1 yellow button, and 1 blue button.
- The right panel, which is a large, squarish panel with a red button and a green button. It is connected to the right of the middle panel.
- The left panel, which is a large, squarish panel with a red and black tile pattern. It is connected to the left of the middle panel.
Between the "left" and the "right" panel, there is a bridge of red cards that connects to the bridge of black cards between the left panel and the middle panel.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
-  Outer Saboteur: Spawns directly below the player.
The 4 hidden stage items are present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.
-  Silver Ring: North of starting area.
-  Metaglio Right: East of starting area.
-  Gold Ring: South of starting area.
-  Metaglio Left: West of starting area.
## Pickups
- 5x  Survarots
- 7x  Rerollos
## Coffin
One  Coffin is located in Ante Chamber.
-  Jimbo: approximately 4 tilesets, northeast of the starting area.
## Waves
Waves in Ante Chamber have the Standard spawn type, meaning enemies appear from all four directions of the player.
Note: As official sources name only a few of the enemies, unit names are mostly made up based on their internal IDs with some creative flair added and may be subject to change.

## Trivia
- The text featured on the stage is gibberish text in Runes.
- The only exception to this would be the area that says "SHOP", which is based on the Shop feature from Balatro.
- Prior to v1.15, either entering this stage, using  Secretino Dagsson, or using any of the Ante Chamber characters would allow for the usage of Survarots. With the introduction of the  Survarocchi, this is no longer the case.
- A part of the stage's floor, thus the Bestiary background for Ante Chamber, have the same pattern as the Checkered Deck in Balatro.

Archon Ascia is an enemy that appears in Dairy Plant, Cappella Magna, and Boss Rash. It has a normal variation, which only appears as a normal enemy in Dairy Plant, and a boss variant, which only appears as a boss.

Archon Spada is an enemy that appears in Gallo Tower and Boss Rash. In Gallo Tower, it only appears as a normal enemy. In Boss Rash, it only appears as a boss.

x1.25x1.9x1.25x1.25 (+0.1 per minute)x1.9 (+0.1 per minute)x1.25 (+0.1 per minute)x1x1.15x1x1.3x1.8x1.3? (edit)+1.1%? (edit)
Astral Stair is the fifth challenge stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by reaching level 80 in Inverse Inlaid Library.
Alternatively, Astral Stair and its Hyper version can both be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "recyclethelibrary".
## Description
A highway used to reach other worlds and dimensions. It's dangerous to venture through alone, but you probably won't listen to me anyway, will you?
## Effects
Enemies grow stronger over time, gaining +10% Max Health and +0.1% Movement Speed every minute. This effect stacks with  Inverse Mode.
Enemies on Astral Stair follow the beat of the music and turn either red or blue. They can only be damaged by weapons of the same color they're currently "wearing". 
Astral Stair is home to special boss enemies,  Cosmic Eggs, which can cast the same  Infinite Corridor ability the player can normally obtain. It halves the player's health at every cast, making it potentially dangerous even to characters with high stat bonuses.

## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this stage is unlocked by default after unlocking the stage.
Hyper mode in Astral Stair has +80% Gold and +10% Luck bonuses.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
-  Phiera Der Tuphello, located in the upper middle room.
-  Eight The Sparrow, located in the lower middle room.
-  Gold Finger, five total, one on each row of the rooms on the stage.
## Relics
Three relics are located in Astral Stair:
-  Trisection, which appears in the bottom room.
-  Chaos Rosalia, which appears in the top room.
-  Astral Stair Map, which appears in the middle room.

## Trivia
- This stage's description a reference to a unique secret that occurs if a US-release PlayStation disk of Castlevania: Symphony of the Night is inserted into a CD player. Initially, Alucard will warn the player to not play the disk as it contains the game's computer data, before humorously relenting by saying, "But you probably won't listen to me anyway, will you?"

Attack Aggressively is the first stage of the adventure "Operation Guns". This stage is available by default.
To unlock the succeeding stage, Bullet Biters, the player has to defeat the Metal Alien.
## Description
Contra Unit, your new orders are in. The president's son has been kidnapped by mercenaries, and they're demanding advanced military technology from Corpo Inc. as ransom - but we don't negotiate with terrorists. You're the only team though enough to take them on. Airdrop into Galuga Jungle, find their base, and blow it the heck up!

Bat Country is the fourth challenge stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by reaching level 80 in Inverse Mad Forest.
Alternatively, Bat Country can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "recycletheforest".
## Description
We can't stop here.
## Effects
As the name of the stage implies, waves are comprised entirely of bat enemies, including many unique variants of  Pipeestrello.
Enemies grow stronger over time on Bat Country, with their Max Health increasing by an additional 10% per minute and their Movement Speed by an additional 1.25% every minute. This effect stacks with  Inverse Mode.
This stage has a -75%  XP bonus, meaning the player actually collects less experience.
 Orologions and  Nduja Fritta Tantos cannot drop from light sources in this stage.
After collecting both  Apoplexy and  Chaos Malachite, three pressure plates labeled "A", "B" and "C" will appear on further visits to the stage, due north of starting area, marked as a "?" on the map. Stepping on them each toggles a background effect; this effect is only visual and has no impact on gameplay.
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this stage is unlocked by default after unlocking the stage.
Hyper mode in Bat Country has +80% Gold and +10% Luck bonuses.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
A small circle of nine  Gold Rings appears to the northeast of the starting area on further visits to the stage after collecting both Relics on this stage. Approaching them will cause 9 Sun Atlanteans to spawn.
The Yellow Sign has no effect on this stage.
## Relics
Two relics are located in Bat Country. Each one of them appears at the center of a large square of Diamonds on a given time and disappears after one minute has passed. When they disappear or are picked up, they can continue to appear on the map and even move on it, even though they are no longer on the field.
-  Apoplexy, which appears at minute 9:00.
-  Chaos Malachite, which appears at minute 18:00.
## Waves
Enemies in Bat Country approach from all four sides of the player.
NOTE: As official sources name only a few of the enemies, unit names are mostly made up based on their internal IDs and are subject to change.

## Tips
- If the player travels to the original location of the ring of  Gold Rings, all nine  Sun Atlanteans will show up nearby, regardless if the player picked up any of the Gold Rings or not. An effective way to collect the Gold Rings while avoiding the Sun Atlanteans is to pick up the  Lavatrix Machina while avoid going to the original location of the ring of Gold Rings.
## Trivia
- This stage bears a striking resemblance to Green Hill Zone, the first level of the 1991 game Sonic the Hedgehog.
- The background visuals and the nine Gold Rings to the southeast are also likely a reference to Sonic the Hedgehog.
- The title and description of this stage are a reference to Fear and Loathing in Las Vegas, a 1971 novel by Hunter S. Thompson.
- This is currently the only stage to have a negative XP bonus by default.
- Bat Country, Tiny Bridge, and Eudaimonia Machine are currently the only stages that the Yellow Sign has no effect on.

# Bat Swarm

| {"type":"Swarm","description":"An instance of a Bat Swarm.","id":"BAT_SWARM","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","image":"Map event-Bat Swarm.jpg","name":"Bat Swarm","release":"27 October 2022","notes":"* Has the Fixed Direction skill.\n* Uses sprites of Little Pipeestrello and Red-eyed Pipeestrello, but does not inherit their properties.","stages":"Mad Forest, Inlaid Library, Boss Rash, and Eudaimonia Machine"}
Bat Swarm is a swarm-type map event that occurs in Mad Forest, Inlaid Library, Boss Rash, and Eudaimonia Machine.
Bat Swarm summons 50  Pipeestrellos that move in a straight line across the map instead of targeting the player. These units push other enemies along their path as they travel. Although they resemble  Little Pipeestrello and  Red-eyed Pipeestrello in appearance, they are a unique variant of Pipeestrello and have distinct properties.
Bat Swarm units are immune to defang.

## Tips
- A good way to clear out most of the Pipeestrello during a Bat Swarm is by using  Garlic, as they generally have low health. This also lets you collect a large number of XP gems at once.

Boss Rash is the third challenge stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by unlocking hyper mode for all 5 normal stages.
Alternatively, Boss Rash can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "peakgamedesign".
## Description
The monsters want entertainment, and if we win their game, the loot is ours for the taking. Let us face these recycled assets and do what we do best... survive!
## Effects
Waves include a lot of bosses from the five main base-game stages (Mad Forest, Inlaid Library, Dairy Plant, Gallo Tower, and Cappella Magna) with increased health. Every minute the enemies' health multiplier increases by ×0.1, without a cap.
Two round pressure plates spawn every 30 second mark until 9:30. The blue pressure plate with an hourglass symbol fast-forwards time to the start of next minute. The red pressure plate with a skull symbol spawns the current wave's bosses again. Bosses that drop an  Arcana Chest do not respawn. Pressure plates disappear at the start of the next minute, if they have not been triggered.
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this map is unlocked by default after unlocking the stage.
Hyper mode in Boss Rash has +50% Gold and +10% Luck bonuses, but it also increases enemy health by 50%.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Pickups
Two  Floor Chicken, two  Orologions, one  Nduja Fritta Tanto and one  Rosary are located outside of the arena. They are entirely inaccessible without the use of Mad Groove (VIII).
## Stage items
All passive items, excluding  Torrona's Box,  Parm Aegis, and  Karoma's Mana, and including the 4 secret passives, are located outside of the arena. They are entirely inaccessible without the use of  Mad Groove (VIII).
## Relics
There is one non-standard relic that players can obtain, assuming the player has obtained the Yellow Sign:
-  Atlas Gate: it will appear at the center of the arena at 7:00.
## Waves
Waves in Boss Rash approach from all four sides of the player.
NOTE: As official sources name only a few of the enemies, unit names are mostly made up based on their internal IDs and are subject to change.

## Trivia
- Before version 1.0, the stage's description was as follows: "Distinguished guests, the moment we've all been itching for is finally here! Let's watch the survivors attempt this challenge of recycled assets!"
- The name of stage "Boss Rash" is a wordplay of game term "boss rush", a situation (an extra challenge stage in this case) in which the player faces all of the bosses of the game, one after another.
- The word "rash" possibly has all meanings applied depending on the context related to "Boss Rash" stage, be it the possibility to reduce time between boss-waves, players suffering from inflammation for playing the game for too long of a period / overly roughly, etc.

Bullet Biters is the second stage of the adventure "Operation Guns". This stage is unlocked after the player has defeated the Metal Alien in the preceding stage, Attack Aggressively.
To unlock the next stage, Neo City Chaos, the player has to survive at least 15 minutes in this stage.
## Description
Rats! These are no mere mercenaries, they're Red Falcon fighters, traitors of the Alien Wars. We thought that group was destroyed, but it seems like they're back from the dead to kidnap the president's nephew - and they've got you surrounded. Survive until we can send a rescue chopper!

Cappella Magna is the fifth stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked after reaching level 80 in Gallo Tower.
Alternatively, Cappella Magna can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "everywhere".
## Description
Conceited were they that ruled from so high yet stooped so low. This nexus of debased purity is the perfect place to find a vampire. Probably.
## Effects
Destructible Stained Glass Windows, marked with a cross, spawn randomly at set locations on the stage. Destroying a window causes it to explode in a pillar of light, dealing damage to nearby enemies.
## Hyper mode
The hyper mode for this map can be unlocked by defeating the Trinacria (25:00) in normal mode.
Hyper mode in Cappella Magna has +90% Gold and +10%  Luck bonuses.
## Overview
Every 5 minutes, the Reapers from the game's previous stages will appear in the order of  The Drowner,  The Stalker, and  The Trickster.
## The Ender
If the player has obtained the Yellow Sign upon starting a run in Cappella Magna, a mysterious yellow hand appears in the bottom right corner of the screen, which removes the  Silver Ring,  Gold Ring,  Metaglio Left, and  Metaglio Right from the map. The  The Maddener will also spawn 10 seconds after the start of the run.
If the player survives to the 30:00 mark, all of the stage's remaining enemies disappear, the Speed-up effect is disabled and the background fades to black. This is followed by a 30 second countdown, before translucent images of  The Reaper,  The Trickster,  The Stalker,  The Drowner, and  The Maddener fly towards the top of the screen and fuse into  The Ender, beginning a special boss fight.
Defeating it unlocks  Game Killer (0) and drops the  Great Gospel.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
-  Crown and  Tirajisú: north of the starting area.
-  Duplicator: south of the starting area.
The four hidden stage items are also present if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign and defeated The Ender.
-  Metaglio Left and  Metaglio Right: west of the starting area.
-  Silver Ring and  Gold Ring: east of the starting area.
## Pickups
- Three  Rosaries can be found south of the starting area, each located one tileset (map tile) away from the previous one.
## Relics
One relic is located in Cappella Magna.
-  Great Gospel: Defeat The Ender.
## Coffin
-  Coffin of  Zi'Assunta Belpaese: south of the starting area.
## Waves
Waves in Cappella Magna approach from all four sides of the player.

## Trivia
- Before version 1.0, the stage was unlocked by completing Holy Forbidden.
- Before version 1.0, the stage's description was as follows: "Have we gone too far? Could have just camped in the forest with all that chicken, but noooo, we had to go chase some random vampire instead."
- Unlike most stage thumbnails, this stage's thumbnail features no enemies. There are no characters or items in it either, except for  Stained Glass.

Carlo Cart is the fifth bonus stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by dealing a total of 25,120 damage with the trains found in Laborratory.
The hyper mode for this stage is unlocked by default.
Alternatively, Carlo Cart can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "thenextstopis".
## Description
Once known as the “King Bible” underground train station, these tunnels beneath the Laborratory now host chaotic, clandestine, and incredibly dangerous go-kart races run by the lizardman King Carlo.
## Effects
The stage in the game features a unique mechanic where the stage scrolls to the right, independent of the player's movement. This movement flips in Inverse mode while "Visually Invert Stages" is enabled, instead scrolling to the left. This creates an impression of the character moving through tracks and enemies appearing to be "racing" with the player. Dropped items and destructibles are directly affected by the scrolling effect, preventing them from being reached. Items like pickups and treasure chests must be caught while still in front of the player. Attempting to move opposite to the stage's movement in order to go after items will be futile as the pace of the scrolling effect counteracts the character's own movement, preventing passed-by items from being reached. Reaching a checkpoint allows items left behind to be gathered.
The scrolling effect also affects enemies, tugging them slightly with the stage's movement, causing them to stutter. This effect becomes more apparent when the player collects an Accelerator pickup. Weapons and passive items spawned by Random Events and certain characters (e.g.,  Santa Ladonna,  Eleanor Uziron [DLC]), and the coffin of a secret character, are unaffected by the stage's effect and remain at the locations they spawned in.
Periodically, a Reaper of each type spawns and actively chases the player, dealing significant damage upon contact. This encourages the player to move forward in order to prevent Reapers from inflicting harm until they are defeated or have despawned over time.
The  Roast Chicken with a Clock in the Middle is disabled in this stage, regardless of whether the player sealed it in their Collection or not.
## Special stage pickups
There are two special "pickup" items that spawn on the tracks of the stage that provide beneficial effects: 
- Accelerator: Touching these accelerates the stage's scrolling and the player's movement by 35% for 5 seconds, without affecting the clock speed. It can be beneficial for fleeing large groups of enemies and quickly reaching items east of the character.
- Checkpoint: It appears occasionally on the stage once every few minutes, it spans the entire vertical area of the track, meaning it cannot be missed or avoided. Once touched, all treasure chests and pickups left behind are moved to the right side of the stage, allowing them to be collected by the player once they reach the screen. Additionally, the player's character receives +5%  Growth,  Luck,  Greed,  Curse,  Move Speed, and  Magnet bonuses for each checkpoint they pass.

## Trivia
- This stage is based on the kart racing game genre and, in particular, the Mario Kart game series.
- The description has a reference to the  King Bible and, indirectly, the Rebibbia underground train station in Rome, Italy.
- The stage actually re-uses the same two tilesets of the map, looping over its entirety. This is evident when using the character  Je-Ne-Viv [DLC], whose ability to "eat" stage boundaries and objects causes destroyed parts of the map to re-appear on a regular basis.
- Specifically, this stage uses the bottom area of Laborratory's map.
- A character with at least +300% Move Speed can effectively move to the left, resisting the stage's scrolling effect.
- Despite a  Dust Elemental enemy being included on the stage's preview banner, in fact, no enemies of that kind spawn in the stage at all.
- In this stage, the  Merchant can be seen running, instead of staying in the same spot, unlike in most other stages.

Castle Grounds is the first stage of the adventure "Legacy of the Moonspell". This stage is unlocked alongside the adventure.
To unlock the succeeding stage, Shrine Valley, the player has to survive at least 1 minute in this stage.
## Description
Decisive Battle Begin!
Miang vs Orochimario!
Can our Shounen heroine muster the mettle to battle the diabolical dragon for more than a minute?

“
Powerful guardian of a race long thought extinct. Its mask represents advanced civilization, mastery of the world, social order, and littering.
— Ars Gouda, p. 96
City Atlantean is an enemy that appears in all normal, bonus and challenge stages. Unlike most enemies, it does not spawn as part a wave or map event, but spawns upon approaching the  Metaglio Left, which is one of the hidden stage items unlocked by obtaining the Yellow Sign.
## Behavior
City Atlantean is resistant to freeze, instant kill effects and debuffs, and it takes reduced knockback. It does not despawn, if it leaves the player's screen, but is instead teleported back, like bosses. When killed, City Atlantean drops a Golden Egg instead of experience.

| {"update":"Patch 0.3.1","name":"Containers","image":"Sprite-Containers.png","stages":"Il Molise, Moongolow","movespeed":"0","release":"12 March 2022","skills":"HP x Level","health":"10 – 60","damage":"0 – 1"}
Containers are enemies that can appear in Il Molise, Moongolow, Lake Foscari [DLC], and Abyss Foscari [DLC]. In Il Molise, they appear as a boss in most waves, and later as a normal enemy. In Moongolow, they appear in an map event lasting throughout the stage, where a cluster of amphoras spawns every 10 seconds at the edges of the screen. In Lake Foscari and Abyss Foscari, they spawn at specific locations, while the pots can be spawn as a pressure plate event.
Containers cannot move and take no knockback. When killed, Anforas and crates drops a Gold Coin instead of experience and is automatically collected. In Moongolow, if the player moves too far from the amphora, it will teleport back into the player's screen.

406040x1.25x1.9x1.25x1.25x1.9x1.25x1x1.15x1x1.2x1.7x2.2+1%+1.1%+1.2%x1x1x3? (edit)x1.25? (edit)
Dairy Plant is the third stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by reaching level 40 in Inlaid Library.
Alternatively, Dairy Plant can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "everywhere".
Reaching level 80 in Dairy Plant while having inverse mode enabled unlocks Mazerella as playable stage.
## Description
The birthplace of Milk Magic churns with chaos. Here, it's not just blood that curdles. A secret map awaits the hero brave enough to enter this overthrown factory.
## Overview
Dairy Plant features an open map with relatively free movement, though large structures resembling industrial facilities, warehouses and pools of milk serve as major obstacles throughout the stage. Enemy spawns occur from all directions, but their spawns can be affected by the layout—if a solid structure blocks the edge of the screen, enemies will not appear from that direction. Two types of destructibles can spawn in the stage. The first are light sources in the form of lamp posts, which drop random pickups when broken. The second are dairy carts, which occasionally appear along rails; once they have taken enough damage, they launch at high speed toward the opposite end of the rail, dealing heavy damage to any enemies they collide with. Trap pizzas, the circular pressure plates found on the ground, can be encountered at set locations throughout the stage. Stepping on one triggers a random map event.
## Hyper mode
The hyper mode for this map can be unlocked by defeating the Sword Guardian boss (25:00) for the first time. It can also be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "everywhere".
Hyper mode in Dairy Plant has +50% Gold and +10% Luck bonuses.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Pickups
-  Green Experience Gem: On the barrel to the right of the player, in the immediate starting area; contains 5 XP.
-  Vacuum: Up to three can be found, all guaranteed to spawn in similar locations.
- By the wall above the player, in the immediate starting area.
- 2 tilesets, south of the starting area.
- 4 tilesets, south of the starting area.
## Stage items
On this stage, 4 unique passive items can be found, each guaranteed to appear. 
-  Attractorb: 4 tilesets, northwest of the starting area.
-  Armor: 2 tilesets, northeast of the starting area.
-  Wings: 2 tilesets, southwest of the starting area.
-  Candelabrador: 4 tilesets, southeast of the starting area.
The 4 hidden stage items are also present if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.
-  Silver Ring: 7 tilesets north of the starting area.
-  Metaglio Right: 7 tilesets east of the starting area.
-  Gold Ring: 7 tilesets south of the starting area.
-  Metaglio Left: 7 tilesets west of the starting area.
## Relics
Two relics are located in Dairy Plant.
-  Milky Way Map: 3 tilesets, south of the starting area.
-  Ars Gouda: 6 tilesets, south of the starting area.
## Coffin
One  Coffin is located in Dairy Plant.
-  Poppea Pecorina: 3 tilesets, southeast of the starting area.
## Waves
Waves in Dairy Plant have the Standard spawn type, meaning enemies appear from all four directions of the player.
NOTE: As official sources name only a few of the enemies, unit names are mostly made up based on their internal IDs with some creative flair added and may be subject to change.

## Traps
Stepping on "trap pizzas," which appear as circular brass plates on the ground, triggers an unavoidable map event selected from 9 predetermined options. Before being activated again, the traps must cool down for at least 20 seconds.

- Before version 1.0, the stage's description was as follows: "The magic map hidden in here might finally lead us to a vampire, or at least to more roast chicken."

Deep Lore is the first stage of the adventure "World of Light and Dark". It is unlocked at the start of this adventure.
If the player survives at least 10 minutes in this stage, they will be able to unlock Inlaid Library, the succeeding stage.
## Description
Imelda has sought a new life in the church - until one night the peace is shattered by a monstrous attack. Backed into a corner, Imelda must call upon her family's power to survive!
## Trivia
- This is the only chapter stage that is exclusive to World of Light and Dark. Besides Planar Bazaar, which is a shop stage that is common to all adventures, every other stage in this adventure can also be played in the main game.

Don't Get Ghosted is the fourth stage of the adventure "Emergency Meeting". To unlock this stage, the player has to defeat a total of 3,000 Moon Ducks in the preceding stage, Ejected.
To unlock the succeeding stage, Space Rocks, the player has to find the  Ghost Lino in this stage.
## Description
Following a colony of space rabbits into an underground cave might not have been the best idea.
I wonder if this creepy cavern is where all the other Crewmates ended up...

Emerald Diorama is a stage in the eponymous DLC of Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by obtaining the  Emerald Disk, which can be bought from  Xanthia in Moongolow for 50,000.
## Description
A miniature replica of a system of interconnected worlds. Emerald Waves guide travellers to their destined paths.
## Overview
Emerald Diorama features several worlds that players can visit under certain circumstances. Note that if the player is playing in Inverse mode with the "Visually Invert Stages" option enabled, the location of each world flips both horizontally and vertically. This is denoted by the "Inverse" directions below. 
| Area | Direction (Normal) | Direction (Inverse) | Stage Item | Boss | Boss Drop | Notes
| The Junction | Central | None | Psychic Ogre | Emerald Rapture | Starting area. Contains 6 portals that leads to 6 other area. The Merchant and Master Librarian are located here.
| Miyako City | North (↑) | South (↓) | Silver Ring Spellbinder | Divine Wood Spirit | Pummarola | Contains a coffin.
| Yomi | North-West (↖) | South-East (↘) | Stone Mask | Specter of Iwanaga-hime | Bullova | 
| Witchdom Pulchra | North-East (↗) | South-West (↙) | Parm Aegis | Earth Dragon | Glaive | 
| Avalon | South-West (↙) | North-East (↗) | Metaglio Left Karoma's Mana | Iron Maiden | Khukuri | 
| Grelon | South-East (↘) | North-West (↖) | Metaglio Right Spinach | Malevolent Door Spirit | Flamberge | 
| Providence | South (↓) | North (↑) | Gold Ring Crown | Living Anguish | Skull O'Maniac | Contains a coffin.
## Hyper mode
This stage's hyper mode can be unlocked by defeating Cursed Monarch, a timed boss who appears at 25:00 in any part of the map.
Activating hyper mode for this stage will increase player speed from +25% to +100%, gold bonus from +25% to 75%, and add a +10% luck bonus.
## Notes
- Miyako City (the north area) is the starting area when entering this stage for the first time.
- In the first playthrough, only one of the 6 portals will open. A parabolic light line will guide the character to the opening portal. The portal to leave each area will close after spawning the boss in the area, and will reopen after defeating the boss.
- When the player has the Visually Invert Stages option enabled, and is playing in Inverse mode, the locations of each biome are flipped both horizontally and vertically. For example, Witchdom Pulchra, which is normally in the North-East, is located to the South-West when both of these options are enabled.
- The map below uses the original locations of each biome and boss.
## Waves

Emergency Meating is the sixth and the final stage of the adventure "Emergency Meeting". To unlock this stage, the player has to find the  Scientist Mina in the preceding stage, Space Rocks.
## Description
The sudden appearance of those three weirdos has interfered with the experiments in the Specimen Room! Some innovative bean-biology project has gone awry, and a bizzare meat creature is summoning swarms of angry aliens. Whatever it is, we're ejecting it!
## Trivia
- According to in-game data, another name for this stage would be "Test Tube Trouble."

Tag, you're it.","dmax":"10","update":"Patch 1.0","image":"","time":"99:00","theme":"The Eudaimonia Machine","dchance":"7.5%","type":"???","adventure":"No","id":"MACHINE","player-speed":"x1.5","version1":"Normal","name":"Eudaimonia Machine","release":"20 October 2022","dchance-max":"50%","version2":"Hyper","version3":"Inverse"}
Eudaimonia Machine (shortened to Eudaimonia M.) is a special stage in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked after obtaining all the standard relics from all other stages. 
The list of required relics includes:
-  Grim Grimoire
-  Ars Gouda
-  Milky Way Map
-  Magic Banger
-  Sorceress' Tears
-  Glass Vizard
-  Mindbender
-  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane
-  Great Gospel
- Yellow Sign
## Description
A space between spaces. The culmination of something, or simply another beginning? Tag, you're it.
## Effects
The Eudaimonia Machine is the final major stage of the game, unlocked by obtaining all the other relics from the other stages. Once it is available, the player can access it at the very top of the level select screen.
Upon starting a run in Eudaimonia Machine, a yellow hand appears at the bottom right of the screen that disables all relics that enable separate game modes and removes all of the current character's  Golden Eggs.
The stage does not spawn any enemies at the start of a run and the player has no choice but to travel upwards, where they will encounter and converse with a mysterious entity who offers a choice. Depending on if the player responds with either "Too Easy" or "Too Hard", they will receive the  Gracia's Mirror or  Seventh Trumpet respectively. The former boots the player back to the starting screen with everything inverted, signifying that Inverse Mode is available. The latter returns them to the starting screen too, but with all the main screen characters absent, signifying that Endless Mode is available.
The player can enter and exit any stage with the newly unlocked mode, and Eudaimonia Machine makes itself available again. Enter it again to converse with the entity, which will then give the player the other relic they did not pick earlier.
Repeat the process with the second mode the player unlocked, and the stage reveals itself again. Entering Eudomania Machine and conversing with the entity again creates text boxes containing garbled text. The entity then states "But enough talk, have at you!" and directly challenges the player as  The Directer, the final boss of the game.
Defeating The Directer unlocks  Greatest Jubilee, and also causes the stage to become permanently unlocked at the end of the stage list, where the player can revisit it to rematch The Directer. This ability to replay is quite useful, as beating The Directer gives over 100,000 gold, and combined with the stone mask, can give upwards of 200,000 gold. As unlocking characters gets more expensive with each character unlocked, replaying the stage is an easy way to get the incredible amounts required.
 Nduja Frita Tantos,  Orologions,  Little Clovers, and  Gilded Clovers cannot drop from light sources in this stage.
## Hyper Mode
Hyper Mode appears to have no effect on this stage.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Relics
Two relics can be obtained in Eudaimonia Machine:
-  Gracia's Mirror
-  Seventh Trumpet
## Trivia
- Eudaimonia is a Greek word that roughly translates to a state of "good spirits" and "happiness".
- If the player reaches level 14 or higher before talking to The Directer, the game will crash. This is currently only achievable with  Sammy [DLC].
- The line, "But enough talk, have at you!", is a direct reference to Dracula's monologue at the beginning of Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.

| {"update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","stages":"Mad Forest","resistances":"-","image":"Sprite-Flower Wall.png","health":"30","movespeed":"20","release":"27 October 2021","skills":"HP x Level","name":"Flower Wall","damage":"1"}
← MudmanFlower WallSkelegem →
“
Said to have first sprouted during a total eclipse of the sun, these blighted blooms appear when they are least needed, which is to say they appear all the time.
— Ars Gouda, p. 9
Flower Wall is an enemy primarily encountered in Mad Forest, with additional appearances in Mt.Moonspell [DLC] and Lake Foscari [DLC].
In Mad Forest, it first appears during the Flower Wall map event and later reappears as a normal enemy in later waves. In Mt.Moonspell and Lake Foscari, it appears during Circle map events and as a special enemy that spawns at specific, fixed locations on the map.
Flower Wall's health scales with the player's level, except for the Foscari variant. Movement speed varies by type: the base variant moves very slowly, the Foscari variant is as fast as most enemies, and both Moonspell variants are immobile. Knockback resistance also differs: the base and Foscari variants take reduced knockback, Moonspell 1 is semi-resistant, and Moonspell 2 is fully resistant.

# Flower Wall (event)
This article is about a map event that spawns flower-like enemies. For the enemy spawned by the event, see Flower Wall. For other uses, see Flower.

| {"type":"Circle","description":"An instance of a Flower Wall.","id":"FLOWER_WALL","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","image":"Map event-Flower Wall.jpg","name":"Flower Wall","release":"27 October 2022","notes":"* Summons Flower Walls that encircle the player.","stages":"Mad Forest"}
Flower Wall is a circle-type map event that occurs in Mad Forest.
The Flower Wall event summons a ring of durable  Flower Wall units around the player, which slowly creeps inward. The Flower Walls trap both the player and enemies inside until they are defeated or expire after their set duration.
## Appearances
| Stage | Minute | Notes
| Mad Forest | 5:00 | Lasts 30 seconds.
| Mad Forest | 10:00 | Lasts 60 seconds.
| Mad Forest | 15:00 | 80% chance, lasts 30 seconds.
| Mad Forest | 25:10 | Lasts 10 seconds, repeats 5 times.
## Tips
- Flower Walls have high health but deal relatively little damage, so it's often better to break through their encirclement rather than stay trapped and risk taking more damage from other enemies in the wave.
## Trivia
- An event similar to the Flower Wall also appears in Mt.Moonspell and Lake Foscari. However, these stages use the generic Circle map event template, which can spawn any enemy type in a ring formation rather than exclusively Flower Walls.

Gallo Tower is the fourth stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by reaching level 60 in Dairy Plant.
Alternatively, Gallo Tower can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "everywhere".
Reaching level 80 in Gallo Tower with inverse mode enabled unlocks Tiny Bridge as playable stage.
## Description
An edifice of science and sorcery where horror was made manifest in the name of progress. Its many artifacts and experiments may lead us to what we seek.
## Hyper mode
The hyper mode for this map can be unlocked by defeating the Giant Enemy Crab (25:00) in normal mode.
Hyper mode in Gallo Tower has +80% Gold and +10% Luck bonuses.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
On this stage, 2 unique passive items are spawned at the start.  Milky Way Map displays the locations of these items. One of each item is guaranteed to appear.
-  Bracer: north of starting area.
-  Spellbinder: south of starting area.
The 4 hidden stage items are also present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.
-  Silver Ring and  Metaglio Left: north of starting area.
-  Gold Ring and  Metaglio Right: south of starting area.
## Relics
Two relics are located in Gallo Tower.
-  Randomazzo: north of starting area.
-  Sorceress' Tears: south of starting area.
## Coffin
The  Coffin of  Concetta Caciotta is located in Gallo Tower. The coffin can be found in a secret room, which can be accessed through a magic mirror that has some runes floating nearby. The magic mirror is located to the south-west of the coffin's location on the map, on the left side of the tower.
## Waves
The stage's vertical layout causes enemies to mostly spawn above and below the screen.
NOTE: As official sources name only a few of the enemies, unit names are mostly made up based on their internal IDs and are subject to change.

For every minute past 30, another Death spawns. The Drowner summoned in this wave cannot be defeated.
## Traps
Stepping on one of the small red circles on the ground triggers an unavoidable map event, chosen at random between 7 predetermined options. The traps have a 20 second cooldown before they can be triggered again.
| Trap events in Gallo Tower
| Event | Unit | Amount | Duration (seconds) | Range | Notes
| Poltergeist Roulette | [Poltergeist]Poltergeist | 50 | 30 | 1 | Poltergeists cannot move and their health is based on the player's level. They trigger self-destruct attack, if the player gets too close, exploding for 50 damage after 2 seconds.
| Poltergeist Roulette | [Poltergeist]Poltergeist | 100 | 30 | 2 | -
| Poltergeist Roulette | [Poltergeist]Poltergeist | 50 | 30 | 2 | -
| Poltergeist Roulette | [Poltergeist]Poltergeist | 50 | 30 | 2 | This event is the same as above, appearing a second time in the list of events.
| Poltergeist Swarm | [Poltergeist]Aggressive Poltergeist | 20 | 30 | 0.7 | Aggressive Poltergeists' health is based on the player's level. They trigger a self-destruct attack, if the player gets too close, exploding for 50 damage after 2 seconds.
| Poltergeist Swarm | [Poltergeist]Aggressive Poltergeist | 60 | 30 | 0.7 | -
| Impefinger Swarm | [Impefinger]Impefinger | 20 | 30 | 0.7 | -
## Trivia
- Before version 1.0, the stage's description was as follows: "This tower hides great magical artifacts and historically accurate monsters."
- This is likely a reference to the E3 2006 showing of Genji: Days of the Blade, a game stated to be based on Japanese history, which also includes a "giant enemy crab." An enemy named as such also appears in the stage.
- There are red runes floating in the background. Those red runes can also be seen with the  Chaos Rune.
## ARG
- Between late October 2025 and early November 2025, poncle ran an alternate reality game that is based on this stage. In this ARG, the community's goal was to discover, interpret, and discuss subtle hints and clues given out by the developers, so that players can submit answers to the password fields across the 12 floors shown in the website https://gallotower.com/.
- After completing the final floor, floor 11, the site then tells the player to cast the spell "guinigigi", which unlocks the weapon  Chaos Rune.

Garlic Paradise is the fifth stage of the adventure "A Garlic Paradise". This stage is unlocked by opening a Jiva Tree in the preceding stage, Rotten Orchard.
If the player reaches at least level 50 in this stage, they will be able to unlock Retirement.
## Description
With the terrifying tree righteously slain, Poe can finally achieve his goal: with the power of the Garlic Paradise, he can amass enough riches to retire!
## Trivia
- Despite there being 10 Garlics and 10 Pummarolas to pick up, the maximum level of the Garlic and Pummarola are 8 and 5, respectively.
- Once the player levels up their Garlic and/or their Pummarola to the maximum level, the rest of the Garlics and Pummarolas will disappear (even if Limit Break is enabled.)

Green Acres is the first challenge stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by unlocking hyper mode for 2 normal stages (for example, Mad Forest and Dairy Plant).
Alternatively, Green Acres can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "dotgogreenacres".
## Description
Fate changes by the minute in a realm where mortals can only trespass. What rewards wait those who challenge its rules? Treasure? Glory? Poultry?
## Effects
The waves that appear in Green Acres are selected at random across all unlocked base maps: Mad Forest, Inlaid Library, Dairy Plant, Gallo Tower, Cappella Magna.
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this map is unlocked by default after unlocking the stage.
Hyper mode in Green Acres has +50% Gold and +10% Luck bonuses, but it also increases enemy health by 50%.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
The 4 hidden stage items are present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.  Milky Way Map displays the locations of these items. One of each item is guaranteed to appear.
-  Silver Ring: north of starting area.
-  Metaglio Right: east of starting area.
-  Gold Ring: south of starting area.
-  Metaglio Left: west of starting area.
## Relics
One relic is located in Green Acres.
-  Magic Banger: north east of starting area.
## Trivia
- Before version v1.0, the stage's description was: "A place where mortals are not allowed. Fate changes every minute."
- The name of this stage may be a reference to the "Red Miles" from Homestuck.

204020x1.35x1.6x1.35x1.25x1.5x1.25x1x1.1x1x1x1.25x1
Hectic Highway is the second stage in the Operation Guns DLC of Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by evolving C-U-Laser.
Alternatively, Hectic Highway can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "hardcorpshighspeed".
## Description
Contra Force, rev your jetbikes – its time to drive fast, and fire faster. Red falcon traitors are invading the C-1 Highway for some treasonable reason, and we need you to defend our crucial infrastructure.
## Effects
Similar to Carlo Cart, this stage has a unique mechanic where the stage itself scrolls to the right, independent of the player's movement, creating an impression of the character moving along the highway and enemies appearing to be "chasing" the player. While playing in Inverse mode with "Visually Invert Stages" enabled, the stage will instead scroll to the left. Aside from its visual effects, dropped items and destructibles are directly affected by the scrolling effect, moving with the stage. Pickups and treasure chests must be "caught" while they are still in front the player and failing to do so results in the items getting left behind. Attempting to move opposite to the stage's movement in order to go after items will be futile as the pace of the scrolling effect counteracts the character's own movement, preventing passed by items from being reached. After a set point, the stage resets, which allows items left behind to be gathered.
Although to a lesser extent, the scrolling effect affects enemies as well, with the stage tugging all enemies slightly as it scrolls, causing enemies to stutter as they oppose the drag with their own movement.
Other elements are unaffected by the stage's scrolling affect, such as weapons or passive items spawned by certain characters (e.g., Santa Ladonna, Eleanor Uziron [DLC] ) or Random Events and the coffin of a secret character found on the stage. These remain at the location they initially appeared and do not move along the stage.
## Waves

Waves in Hectic Highway have the Horizontal spawn type, meaning enemies appear from the left and right sides of the player.

Hidden Cave is the fourth stage of the adventure "Legacy of the Moonspell". This stage is unlocking by opening Syuuto Moonspell's coffin in the preceding stage, Forbidden Peaks.
To unlock the succeeding stage, Frozen Lake, the player has to defeat Megalo Syuuto Moonspell in this stage.
## Description
Catastrophe strikes- the cave floor crumbles and Syuuto is overcome by the slight inconvenience, metamorphosing into his Oni form! Trapped in this hidden chamber, you must tame Megalo Syuuto's unleashed power to survive.
## Trivia
- Aside from Planar Bazaar, this is the only stage in the "Legacy of the Moonspell" adventure that has slightly different modifiers than the rest of the stages (excluding the time limit). The difference between this one and the other stages is that this stage has a higher player movement speed than the rest of the stages.
- The modifiers for gold and luck (only in Hyper Mode) remain the same compared to the rest of the stages, though.

This isn't right...","enemy-speed2":"x1.25","player-speed2":"x1.25","dmax":"100","update":"Patch 0.6.1","enemy-health":"x1","version2":"Hyper","image":"","time":"5:00","starting-spawns2":"50","enemy-health2":"x1","enemy-speed":"x1.25","theme":"Before Concession X","dchance":"100%","enemy-min2":"x1","type":"Hidden Ground","luck":"x1","adventure":"No","id":"STAGEX","starting-spawns":"50","player-speed":"x1.25","version1":"Normal","projectile-speed":"x1","version3":"Inverse","name":"Holy Forbidden","greed":"x1","release":"23 May 2022","dchance-max":"100%","enemy-min":"x1","greed2":"x1"}
Holy Forbidden is a hidden stage in Vampire Survivors. It becomes temporarily accessible in Moongolow after defeating the special angel-like boss that spawns at the 14 minute mark during the Lunar Eclipse Event. It is unlocked in stage selection after completing the event for the first time. After obtaining the Yellow Sign, this stage is hidden and can no longer be accessed by stage selection or the event (it can still be accessed by casting the spell “iwanttoseeitagain” using the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane).
## Description
Wait, you can see this too? This isn't right...
## Effects
Upon entering the stage the player loses their Revivals, Armor, Move Speed, Growth, Arcana, passive items and weapons, except their starting weapon. If the player's starting weapon has evolved, such as the Garlic into the Soul Eater, it is changed back into its base form from the start, Garlic at Level 1. The player is approached by a wave of bats in a narrow corridor floating in the sky. The game cannot be paused and Hurry Mode has no effect. After 15 seconds the sky in the background turns red, the UI disappears and the bats turn into angelic beings.
Some enemies drop experience, however, the player cannot level up here. Light sources are created along the stage set locations, but they replenish at a much slower rate and only drop Coins, Coin Bags, Nduja Fritta Tantos, Little Clovers and Floor Chicken.
The player must make their way through the corridor and pick up the Rosary to banish the hordes of enemies. The final wall to the right of the Rosary is false and the player can walk on an invisible bridge to unlock the Yellow Sign. The stage also automatically ends 30 seconds after picking up the Rosary if the player chooses not to pick up the Yellow Sign, or dies.
## Items
## Pickups
One  Rosary is lying on the ground at the end of the corridor. Picking it up will end the onslaught and reveal The Maddener, which then flees.
## Relics
One relic is located in Holy Forbidden.
- Yellow Sign: east of starting area.
After obtaining the Yellow Sign, the game returns to the warning screen with the text being randomized, and the stage can no longer appear and be selected.
## Hyper Mode
This map cannot be accessed in Hyper Mode.
## Waves
Other than Bats/Bells, enemies in Holy Forbidden are spawned in a special sequence more akin to events than waves.
NOTE: As official sources name only a few of the enemies, unit names are mostly made up based on their internal IDs and are subject to change.

## Tips
- Characters eligible to access this sequence are indicated by a glowing blood moon appearing on Moongolow's titlecard on the Stage Selection screen.
- Disable the Curse Power Up to make things easier. If you cannot disable it because it's not max level, try getting it to max level to disable it, or refund your Power Ups to reset it back to level 0.
- Since there are close to zero Experience Gem drops, the temporary starting bonuses at level 1 make Porta Ladonna and Suor Clerici good candidates for a successful run.
- The lightning is a bit random though, though the big Santa Water AoEs can easily kill the Bell Angel Bombers while the regeneration covers what little hits one should take.
- Destroy as many light sources as you can. Due to the reduced item pool, there is slightly higher concentration of Floor Chicken and Nduja Fritta Tantos, allowing you to sustain yourself and fend off enemies.
## Trivia
- The name and design of this area is a reference to Hidden Forbidden Holy Ground, an important location in the .hack series.
- When Holy Forbidden appears in stage selection, its stage image displays vertically flipped and there is some animated static in it.
- While trying to access the stage by using the spell, the player will enter with the currently selected character; however if Sir Ambrojoe, Iguana Gallo Valletto, Divano Thelma, Zi'Assunta Belpaese, Queen Sigma or any secret character is selected, the player will enter with Antonio Belpaese instead.
- This is likely due to the fact that after 15 seconds, the player character swaps to the Stage Killer color pallete, and those do not exist for most other characters.
- It is possible for any character to enter Holy Forbidden via a glitch. This process to do this is by using the spell “iwanttoseeitagain”, then dying before completing the stage, then picking any character to play as, going to the stage selection, exiting out, and then finally casting either “onjupiter” or “andmars”. This will allow the selected character to enter Holy Forbidden.

Ice Cream is the second stage of the adventure "To End An Ice Age". This stage is unlocked by opening the coffin that unlocks Mortaccio in the preceding stage, Ice Age.
If the player opens the coffin in this stage, they can unlock Bianca Ramba, as well as the succeeding stage, A Fiery Apparition.
## Description
Mortaccio's rattling bones make funny noises as he walks with immortal determination, leading She-Moon towards the ruins of a forsaken factory. Is this skeleton moved by the flame of a long lost love?

Il Molise is the first bonus stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by unlocking hyper mode for any normal stage.
Alternatively, Il Molise can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "relaxenjoylife".
## Description
The flowers seem to sing here, calling out to weary heroes. Is such an unspoilt place even real? Does it matter? Is there roast chicken?
## Overview
Il Molise is an open grassy map that mainly features Molisano enemies (excluding  The Reaper after the time limit and the  Bat Dragon, discussed below). Molisano enemies have a few distinct characteristics that differentiate them from most enemies found in other stages:
- They are immobile and only deal damage if the player's character touches them.
-  O'Sole Meeo, in particular, does not take damage from Molisano enemies.
- They gain more health when touched by the  Celestial Dusting.
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this map is unlocked by default after unlocking the stage.
Hyper mode in Il Molise has +50% Gold and +20% Luck bonuses, but it also increases enemy health by 60%.
## Bat Dragon
If the player has obtained the  Green Eyes Vizard relic, they can encounter a  Bat Dragon in this stage. Its position in the  Milky Way Map is indicated by a [A sprite icon representing Question Mark Black with no link] black question mark. It is located 5-6 tilesets northeast of the starting area.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
The 4 hidden stage items are present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.  Milky Way Map displays the locations of these items. One of each item is guaranteed to appear.
-  Silver Ring: north of starting area.
-  Metaglio Right: east of starting area.
-  Gold Ring: south of starting area.
-  Metaglio Left: west of starting area.
## Waves
The enemies in Il Molise cannot move and can only spawn outside the screen in a tiled layout. The player has to move to make space for more enemies to spawn.
NOTE: As official sources name only a few of the enemies, unit names are mostly made up based on their internal IDs and are subject to change.

## Tips
- Il Molise can be unlocked by typing "relaxenjoylife" while in main menu.
- Unlike normal stages, the Reaper appears at the 15 minute mark.
- At the 9, 10, 11 and 13 minute marks, the bosses spawned will always drops at least a one item chest. These chests can exceptionally evolve multiple weapons at once if the received chest is a higher tier, three or five items chest, which have 1% and 2% base chances to drop respectively.
- Chests containing Arcanas at 11 and 21 minute marks only drop an Arcana, however.
- After 15 minutes, enemies are erased and the Reaper arrives. Only some gold pots spawn sparsely at declining rate. After 21 minutes however, the pots start to spawn at high rate until the end of the minute and cover the screen as the character moves. This is a great way to farm Gold as each pot is guaranteed drop one coin.
- Curse has little to no benefit in Il Molise due to the screen already being constantly filled to the enemy maximum and its health increase only making enemies harder to kill.
- Having a high amount of Magnet (approximately 400+) will cause the enemies to cease spawning correctly, only appearing off-screen at the far corners in the direction of movement. While this is not possible on most characters, it can be attained with a combination of the Attractorb and a high enough starting Magnet stat on missingN▯.
- Even though the freeze property is not as effective as it is when it is used in other stages, given that the enemies in this stage are already immobile, it can still be useful for the player to prevent taking melee damage from the enemies in this stage.
## Trivia
- Il Molise refers to a region in Southern Italy of the same name; its use in the game to reference a barren place can be attributed to the Italian meme "il Molise non esiste" (Molise doesn't exist), of difficult attribution. The region, of course, actually exists.
- Before version v1.0, the stage's description was as follows: "There exists places that don't exist. Come to relax and enjoy life. See you in the country."
- The stage's theme is an instrumental remix of the song "Funiculì, Funiculà".
- If the spell 'whathappensifisitidleforoneday' is cast, the player will be taken to the stage and the timer will be set to 1440:00, 1440 minutes being exactly one day. Trying to enter this way may also softlock you on the loading screen with the music and a sound effect playing.

Illusory World is the fifth stage in the adventure Legacy of the Bloodmoon. This stage is unlocked by beating Congregation in the preceding stage, Wicked Maze
If the player defeats Megalo Baalthasar, they will be able to unlock the succeeding stage, Room 1665.
## Description
Step between the angles of the aberrant architecture into the immaterial anomaly at the heart of the Manor. The Red Moon is risen within Baal'Thasar, accursed progenitor of the Bloodmoon Clan, writing beyond time, space, and reality.
## Overview
Illusory World is a small map shaped like an inverted T. To the left is one  Empty Tome and four  Crowns. To the right is one  Duplicator and four  Armors. Above, there is an arcana chest and the coffin. Opening the coffin will stop the time and begin the boss fight against Baal'Thasar begins.
During the fight the timer is paused and the player is given SHEATHED POWER LAW which will ? (edit). Dimension tears from  Blacken Firmament will appear on the screen and damage the player if touched. Occasionally, a black hole from  Damnation will attract and destroy items, enemies, and the player. If the player caught in the black hole, their most recently acquired weapon will be destroyed.
Upon defeating Megalo Baalthasar, the sixth stage, Room 1665, is unlocked and White Hand will appear to kill the player. The time is set at 26 minutes.

Inlaid Library is the second stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by reaching level 20 in Mad Forest.
Alternatively, Inlaid Library can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "everywhere".
Reaching level 80 in Inlaid Library while having inverse mode enabled unlocks Astral Stair as playable stage.
## Description
The Futile Scribe was cursed to know all and understand nothing. His endless library may gift us insight... or damnation. A mask of stone whispers endlessly within.
## Overview
Inlaid Library features a map with a long hallway layout, bordered by bookshelves along the top and bottom that restricts movement to left and right directions. Enemy spawns are similarly limited by the layout, allowing to only appear from west and east ends of the map. Obstacles like small tables, bookshelves, and pianos can be regularly found on the map, which can be easily navigated around without significantly obstructing the player's movement. The only destructibles that spawn in this stage are light sources in the form of candelabras, which will drop random pickups once broken.
## Hyper mode
The hyper mode for this stage can be unlocked by defeating the Hag (25:00) for the first time. It can also be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "everywhere".
Hyper mode in Inlaid Library has +50% Gold and +10% Luck bonuses.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Pickups
- One  Green Experience Gem: At the table north of the player in the starting area; contains 5 XP.
## Stage items
On this stage, 2 unique passive items can be found.
-  Empty Tome: Up to four may appear with 40%, 30%, and 20% chance for each additional Empty Tome to spawn.
- 5 tilesets, west of the starting area. (Guaranteed to spawn)
- 6 tilesets, west of the starting area.
- 7 tilesets, west of the starting area.
- 8 tilesets, west of the starting area.
-  Stone Mask: 5 tilesets, east of the starting area.
The 4 hidden stage items are also present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.
-  Silver Ring: 7 tilesets, east of the starting area.
-  Metaglio Right: 9 tilesets, east of the starting area.
-  Gold Ring: 7 tilesets, west of the starting area.
-  Metaglio Left: 9 tilesets, west of the starting area.
## Relics
One relic is located in Inlaid Library. 
-  Grim Grimoire: 4 tilesets, west of the starting area.
## Coffin
One  Coffin is located in Inlaid Library.
-  Giovanna Grana: 6 tilesets, east of the starting area.
## Waves
Because of the layout of the stage, enemies will spawn from the left and right sides of the screen, often approaching in roughly vertical crowds. 
NOTE: As official sources name only a few of the enemies, unit names are mostly made up based on their internal IDs with some added flavor and are subject to change.

## Trivia
- The name of this stage is a reference to the Long Library, an area in Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.
- Before version 1.0, the stage's description was as follows: "This quiet, long library is the ideal place where to rest, meditate, and forage afor roast chicken. But what's a stone mask doing here?"
- If the player clips through the top or bottom walls and travels far enough, they will reach another hallway, giving the impression that the stage is formatted like a non-linear map.
- Interestingly, these alternate areas do not spawn any enemies, and enemies from the main area cannot reach the player in these sections, including The Reaper.

Jellyfish Swarm is a map event that appears in Dairy Plant. It summons a large group of jellyfish, which slowly travels across the screen.
Swarm Jellyfish have fixed movement, instead of homing to the player, and take reduced knockback.

Jellyfish Wall is a map event that appears in the Dairy Plant and Laborratory. It summons 40 Wall Jellyfish by default, forming an ellipsoid ring around the player, lasting for 30 seconds. The ring of jellyfish periodically closes in on and moves away from the player, keeping the player and enemies contained within its perimeter.
The Wall Jellyfish takes 80% less knockback than regular Jellyfish.

Kappa is an enemy that appears in Mt.Moonspell. They are located at the frozen lake in the northwest of the map near the  Attractorb and the river running along the south of the map near the  Gold Ring.
Killing 6,000 Kappas is the requirement for unlocking  Gav'Et-Oni ★.

## Trivia
- This enemy is based on the kappa, a turtle-like yōkai that lives in bodies of water. It is commonly depicted as being hostile towards humans, especially children, but can be rendered harmless towards a person if they offer it a cucumber with their name written onto it.

Laborratory is the seventh challenge stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by collecting a total of 33  Rosaries.
The hyper mode for this stage is unlocked by default. 
Alternatively, Laborratory can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "divineassault".
## Description
Formerly the foundry where the famed anti-vampiric weaponologist Santa Ladonna created many trinkets, the Laborratory now lies abandoned. Whispers echo that Santa fled from this makeshift workshop when her experimental alchemy somehow opened a door that was never supposed to be breached.
## Effects
This stage features interactable rooms, winding corridors, and a train track found by going south. Each area has its own effects, which could be to the player's benefit or detriment. Levers are a stage prop that can be found throughout the stage, which, depending on Luck may spawn weapons, passive items, or swarms of tough enemies that rush by the player.  Silver Chests can be dropped by bosses before 10:00.
## Levers
On this stage, levers appear at different locations on the map. Walking over these levers causes them to flip over, resulting in various effects, which include spawning a pickup, weapon, passive item, or enemy wave.
Some lever effects are unique to the location where they are found. Below are the possible locations where a lever can spawn.
- Central Room: The largest room and the nearest to the player's initial location. Located by going north of the central area (south in inverse). The levers found here may produce the following:
-  Santa Javelin /  Arma Dio
-  Metaglio Left /  Metaglio Right
- Swarm of Yellow Reaper Trainees / Trinacria / Fallen Cherubbello (self-destructing)
- Danger Room: This room is located west of the central area (east in inverse) and is characterized by the danger signs on the wall in front of the room. The levers found here may produce the following:
-  Cross /  King Bible
-  Clover /  Spellbinder
- Swarm of Jellyfish / Dust Elementals / Musc Muscs
- Electricity Room: This room is located east of the central area (west in inverse) and is characterized by the high voltage warning signs displayed on the front of the room, which can also be found on the walls of the corridor leading to this room. The levers found here may produce the following:
-  Santa Water
-  Attractorb /  Silver Ring /  Gold Ring
- Swarm of Jellyfish / Dust Elementals / Musc Muscs
- Near the Railroad: The railroad located south of the stage—specifically, the walkway below the rails, and the platform by the corridors—frequently spawns levers that differ from levers found elsewhere in that they summon a train called Take Us Away rather than items or enemies. This train damages any enemy that it collides with on its path.
Additionally, levers may spawn pickups that are not room-specific and can spawn in any room type (not including the levers near the railroad).
Levers can spawn at the aforementioned locations off-screen, once every 20 in-game seconds. Each room may spawn up to 5 levers at once, but the area by the railroad can have levers up to the max destructible count of the stage (which is 12). The favorability of an effect and the spawn rate of the levers are affected by Luck.
The effect of Mad Groove (VIII) does not work on levers.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Pickups
Levers found inside rooms can spawn a Coin Bag, Vacuum, Rosary, or Little Clover.
## Stage Items
These items can be found by flicking the levers found inside rooms. Refer to the previous section for their locations.
## Weapons
-  Cross
-  King Bible
-  Santa Water
-  Santa Javelin
-  Arma Dio
## Passive items
-  Clover
-  Attractorb
-  Spellbinder
The 4 hidden stage items can also be found here, which can be found without the Yellow Sign unlocked.
-  Silver Ring
-  Metaglio Right
-  Gold Ring
-  Metaglio Left

## Traps
Stepping on "trap pizzas," which appear as circular plates on the ground, triggers an unavoidable map event, selected from 9 predetermined options. These traps can be discovered inside rooms, in front of lever spawn points, or along passageways leading to other rooms. The traps require a minimum cooldown of 15 seconds before they can be activated again.
| Trap events in Laborratory
| Event | Unit | Amount | Duration (seconds) | Range | Notes

| Jellyfish Swarm | [Jellyfish] | Swarm Jellyfish | 50 | 20 | - | -
## Trivia
- This is presently the only stage in the game where the hidden stage items can be found without having the Yellow Sign unlocked.

Lake Foscari is the first stage in the Tides of the Foscari DLC for Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked upon owning the DLC.
## Description
An enchanted forest stuffed with fae mysteries and mythological entities. The perfect place to have an average adventure with zero complications.
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this stage can be unlocked by defeating the Avatar of Gaea (25:00) in normal mode.
Hyper mode in Lake Foscari has a +100% Gold and +10% Luck bonus.
## Items
Lake Foscari map with full item spawn locations.
The following items can be found in this stage:
- The map has a green crystal (Seal of the Lake) that has to be destroyed by Keitha Muort and her evolved base weapon. It teleports Keitha to Abyss Foscari and unlocks the stage.
## Stage items
-  Armor: 10%, 10%, 10%, 10% chance for more Armor to spawn. Northeast of the starting area in the forest maze.
-  Bracer: 10%, 10%, 10%, 10% chance for more Bracer to spawn. North of the starting area in the mushroom field with brown soil.
-  Clover: 10%, 10%, 10%, 10% chance for more Clover to spawn. Northwest of the starting area in a field of flowers.
-  Crown: 10%, 10%, 10%, 10% chance for more Crown to spawn. North of the starting area in the hedge maze.
-  Skull O'Maniac: 10%, 10%, 10%, 10% chance for more Skull O'Maniac to spawn. East of the starting area in the forest maze.
-  Academy Badge
- Available after Keitha has broken the Seal of the Lake northeast of the starting area and located there.
-  Arcana Treasure Chest: located northwest of the starting area in the mushroom field with brown soil.
The 4 hidden stage items are also present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.
-  Silver Ring: north of starting area.
-  Metaglio Right: northeast of starting area.
-  Gold Ring: southeast of starting area.
-  Metaglio Left: northwest of starting area.
## Pickups
- 4x  Little Clover:
- Three are west of the starting area, each Little Clover is surrounded by 5 Experience Gems
- One is east of the starting area in a hidden path near the Skull O'Maniac spawn locations.
- 15x  Blue Experience Gems: west of the starting area, 5 Experience Gems each surround one of the three Little Clovers.
-  Rosary: north of the starting area in the hedge maze near the Crowns
-  Orologion: north of the starting area in the village.
-  Nduja Fritta Tanto:
- One northwest of the starting area near the Arcana treasure chest.
- One east of the starting area in a hidden path with the Rich Coin Bag and Vacuum
- 2x  Rich Coin Bag: east of the starting area in a hidden path with the Nduja Fritta Tanto and Vacuum.
- 4x  Floor Chicken: north of the starting area in the village.
## Relics
One relic is located in Lake Foscari.
-  Lake Foscari Map: northeast of starting area.
## Coffin
The  Coffin of  Eleanor Uziron is located in the far northwest of the starting area.

After a while, the enemy minimum is increased to 150; it starts to spawn its own Ghostly Apparitions 5 seconds after that; and it also stops being invulnerable. Different parts of the stage have different enemies.

| Nutmeg | WIP
| Scaling Fish-tailed Njuggles | WIP
| Scaling Sea Trow | WIP
| Scaling Hill Trow | WIP
| Fungoman | To the north-west of the starting area with a layer of humus (browner ground).
| Foscari Mantichana | To the north-west of the starting area in the forest ; or directly south-west of the small maze in the middle
| Foscari Flower Wall | WIP
| Foscari Crate | WIP
| Foscari Pot | WIP
## Traps
Stepping on one of the red flower in the middle of the map on the ground triggers an unavoidable map event, chosen at random between 9 predetermined options. The traps have a 20 second cooldown before they can be triggered again.
