 "Forest Night Fever" "Forest Night Fever" "The Beginning"101510x1.1x2x1.1x1.1x2x1.1 (+0.005 per minute)x1x1.25x1x1x1.5x3+0%+0%+0.2%x1x1x3 (+0.05 per minute)x1x1.25x1
Mad Forest is the first stage in Vampire Survivors, unlocked from the very start of the game. It is the only stage that is available in the demo version of the game on itch.io, the others being completely inaccessible, rather than being locked like in the full version of the game.
Reaching level 80 in Mad Forest with inverse mode enabled unlocks Bat Country as playable stage.
Boon Marrabbio (enemy), which is a special enemy, will spawn under certain conditions in Mad Forest after obtaining Yellow Sign.
## Description
Once a thriving haven, now a dumping ground for evil. A vampire is said to be the root of this evil, but we can find only mayhem and roast chicken.
## Overview
Mad Forest features an open map with a large area and very few obstacles, giving players plenty of space to move around. The only destructible that spawn in this stage are light sources in the form of braziers, which will drop random pickups once broken.
## Hyper mode
The hyper mode for this stage can be unlocked by defeating the Giant Blue Venus (25:00) for the first time in normal mode. It can also be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "everywhere".
Hyper mode in Mad Forest has a +50% Gold bonus.
## Bat Dragon
If the player has obtained the  Green Eyes Vizard relic, they can encounter a  Bat Dragon in this stage. Its position in the  Milky Way Map is indicated by a [A sprite icon representing Question Mark Black with no link] black question mark. It is located ~1.5 tilesets northwest of the starting area.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
On this stage, 5 unique passive items can be found.
-  Skull O'Maniac: 6 tilesets, northwest of starting area.
-  Hollow Heart: Between 4 and 5 tilesets, north of starting area.
-  Spinach: Up to four may appear with 30%, 20%, and 10% chance for each additional Spinach to spawn.
- 2 tilesets northeast and 3 tilesets, east of the starting area (guaranteed to spawn).
- 2 tilesets northeast and 5 tilesets, east of the starting area.
- 2 tilesets southeast and 4 tilesets, east of the starting area.
- 2 tilesets southeast and 6 tilesets, east of the starting area.
-  Pummarola: 5 tilesets, south of starting area.
-  Clover: Up to four may appear with 30%, 20%, and 10% chance for each additional Clover to spawn.
- 3 tilesets southwest and 4 tilesets, west of the starting area (guaranteed to spawn).
- 4 tilesets southwest and 2 tilesets, west of the starting area.
- 4 tilesets southwest and 4 tilesets, west of the starting area.
- 3 tilesets southwest and 5 tilesets, west of the starting area.
The 4 hidden stage items are also present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.
-  Silver Ring: 7 tilesets, north of the starting area.
-  Metaglio Right: 7 tilesets, east of the starting area.
-  Gold Ring: 7 tilesets, south of the starting area.
-  Metaglio Left: 7 tilesets, west of the starting area.
## Coffin
One  Coffin is located in Mad Forest.
-  Pugnala Provola: 6 tilesets, northeast of the starting area.
## Waves
Waves in Mad Forest have the Standard spawn type, meaning enemies appear from all four directions of the player.
NOTE: As official sources name only a few of the enemies, unit names are mostly made up based on their internal IDs with some creative flair added and may be subject to change.

## Trivia
- Mad Forest has the second lowest Move Speed bonuses compared to every other stage in the game, even including Adventure maps, with only Eudaimonia Machine having a lower Move Speed.
- On the other hand, it has higher bonuses to Speed and Move Speed in its hyper mode.
- Mad Forest is also the only stage that does not grant a Luck bonus in its hyper mode.
- The name of this stage is a reference to the song "Mad Forest," which is the background theme of the Forest of Darkness in Castlevania III: Dracula's Curse.
- Before version 1.0, the stage's description was as follows: "The castle is a lie, but there's still free roast chicken here, so it's all good."

← Giant BatMantichanaBig Mummy →
“
Gallo Tower’s early horticultural experiments yielded nought but horrifying mutants. These oversized mantids were bred in Lab 22 and subsequently dumped in the Mad Forest when it became apparent they couldn’t be trained and tasted terrible.
— Ars Gouda, p. 35
Mantichana is an enemy primarily encountered in Mad Forest, with additional appearances in Boss Rash and Lake Foscari [DLC].
In Mad Forest, it first appears as a boss and later reappears as a normal enemy in later waves. In Boss Rash, it appears exclusively as a boss. In Lake Foscari, it appears during Circle map events and also as a special enemy that spawns at a specific, fixed location on the map.
Mantichana is semi-resistant to knockback. Its boss and Foscari variants have health that scales with the player's level, with the boss variant also being partially resistant to freeze.

Mazerella is a stage in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching level 80 in Dairy Plant while having Inverse Mode enabled.
Alternatively, Mazerella can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "recycletheplant".
## Description
A lactose labyrinth lies buried in the buttery basement of the Dairy Plant. Home to minotaurs and governed by Trickle-down milkonomics. Grabbing the bull by the horns and cheesing it through this mad maze is the only winning formula.
## Effects
Enemies grow stronger over time, gaining +10% Max Health and +0.1% Movement Speed every minute. This effect stacks with  Inverse Mode.
Mazerella is home to enemies that charge at the player, instead of simply running at them.
Upon starting the stage, two invulnerable dancers spawn on either side of the player. These dancers will pick up any item they run into, including Relics,  Treasure Chests, and  Experience Gems. To defeat these dancers, the player must chase them to specific rooms on both sides of the map, in which the dancers will become vulnerable and will drop all of their items upon death.
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this stage is unlocked by default after unlocking the stage.
Hyper mode in Mazerella has +80% Gold and +10% Luck bonuses.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
-  Axe
-  Knife
-  Clock Lancet
-  Laurel
-  Armor (×2)
-  Wings (×3)
-  Attractorb (×3)
-  Candelabrador (×3)
-  Carréllo (×2)
-  Arma Dio
## Relics
Two relics can be obtained in Mazerella:
-  Chaos Lazulia: west of the starting area.
-  Wax Fetish: east of the starting area.

## Trivia
- "Mazerella" is a portmanteau of following words:
- maze (for bewilderment and labyrinth).
- "Maze" context wise also references the Labyrinth from Ancient Greek mythology. The Labyrinth served as a maze to contain the Minotaur, which this stage as well as the Dairy Plant primarily feature as enemies. Additionally, a Colossus can be found in the center of Mazerella that contributes to a secret unlock, which possibly serves as a direct reference to the Minotaur itself (alongside The Colossi from the game "Shadow of the Colossus".
- Mozzarella cheese
- Tarantella, a southern Italian folk dance popularly believed to be the therapy method for tarantism which also was believd to be result from the bite of the wolf spider Lycosa tarantula.

Meeting Called is the first stage of the adventure "Emergency Meeting". This stage is unlocked with the adventure.
To unlock the succeeding stage, Suspect Security, the player will have to evolve the  Report! with the  Mini Crewmate to get the  Emergency Meeting.
## Description
Red has been called to an Emergency Meeting, but nobody else is here... what's going on?
Why have the doors locked? And were there always so many bins in the meeting room?
Someone needs to report this mess!

- The name is a portmanteau between the word "minotaur" and the Italian word "mignotta", which means "whore".

“
Powerful guardian of a race long thought extinct. Its mask represents the Moon, bringer of tides, object of mystery, and something to look at when you’re bored at night.
— Ars Gouda, p. 95
Moon Atlantean is an enemy that appears in all normal, bonus and challenge stages. Unlike most enemies, it does not spawn as part a wave or map event, but spawns upon approaching the  Silver Ring, which is one of the hidden stage items unlocked by obtaining the Yellow Sign.
## Behavior
Moon Atlantean is resistant to freeze, instant kill effects and debuffs, and it takes reduced knockback. It does not despawn, if it leaves the player's screen, but is instead teleported back, like bosses. When killed, Moon Atlantean drops a Golden Egg instead of experience.

Moongolow is the second bonus stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by unlocking hyper mode for four normal stages.
Alternatively, Moongolow can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "honesty".
## Description
Legend tells of a city swallowed by the sea under a full moon's callous watch. Home to mysteries unknown, we ought to see what lies within its walls.
## Effects
This stage starts with most of the passive items in the game available as floor pickups surrounding the spawn area. The  Merchant also spawns here, regardless if the player has the  Glass Vizard.
If the player has installed any of the following DLC, they will be able to find  Xanthia (the merchant from Planar Bazaar) on the left side of the map. This merchant offers special items:
- If the player installed the Emerald Diorama DLC, she offers the  Emerald Disk, which costs 50,000. If the player obtains this item, they will be able to access the Emerald Diorama stage.
- If the player installed the Ante Chamber DLC, she offers the  Joker Disk, which costs 50,000. If the player obtains this item, they will be able to access the Ante Chamber stage.
## Amphora clusters
 Moon Anforas periodically spawn on the ground, dropping a Gold Coin when they are destroyed and is automatically collected. Every 10 seconds, up to 18 amphoras can be spawned just outside the screen, the amount depending on the players  Luck:
amount=6⋅totalLuck
Amphoras start despawning if more than 100 are on the stage at a time. Amphoras do not despawn if the player moves too far from them but are teleported back to the edges of the screen instead.
## Lunar Eclipse Event
When entering Moongolow with one of the following characters: 
-  Antonio Belpaese,  Imelda Belpaese,  Pasqualina Belpaese,  Gennaro Belpaese,  Arca Ladonna,  Porta Ladonna,  Lama Ladonna,  Poe Ratcho,  Suor Clerici,  Dommario,  Krochi Freetto, Christine Davain,  Pugnala Provola,  Giovanna Grana,  Poppea Pecorina,  Concetta Caciotta,  Mortaccio,  Yatta Cavallo,  Bianca Ramba,  O'Sole Meeo
The player will experience a special sequence of events. During this event (dubbed the lunar eclipse event), the background will gradually turn red and the music will become detuned. Different sets of enemies will spawn at the 12th minute mark onward. At the 14th minute mark, a fish-eye effect is applied to the screen, a large amount of eyeballs begin circling the player, and a special, angel-like boss is spawned. If the boss is defeated, the screen will become increasingly distorted, and after a minute, the game will "freeze". After a couple of seconds, the player will unlock and automatically teleport to Holy Forbidden. After clearing Holy Forbidden, the eclipse event will be disabled.
Whether a character is eligible to access this sequence is indicated by a glowing blood moon that appears on Moongolow's title card during stage selection.
Note that clearing Holy Forbidden will not unlock any normal unlocks for the run (even if the player normally qualifies for them). Only unlocks related to the Yellow Sign and the eclipse will be unlocked.
## The Healer
See Healer.
## The Eggman
See Eggman.
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this stage is unlocked by default after unlocking the stage.
Hyper mode in Moongolow has +50% Gold and +20% Luck bonuses, but it also increases enemy health by 60%.
## Items
Following items can be found on ground in Moongolow:
## Stage items
On this stage, all 16 unique non-DLC passive items excluding  Torrona's Box,  Parm Aegis, and  Karoma's Mana are spawned at the start.  Milky Way Map displays the locations of these items. One of each item is guaranteed to appear.
- Southwest of starting area:
-  Hollow Heart
-  Pummarola
-  Armor
-  Wings
- Northeast of starting area:
-  Spinach
-  Bracer
-  Spellbinder
-  Candelabrador
- Southeast of starting area:
-  Empty Tome
-  Duplicator
-  Tiragisú
-  Attractorb
- Northwest of starting area:
-  Clover
-  Crown
-  Stone Mask
-  Skull O'Maniac
The 4 hidden stage items are also present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.
-  Silver Ring: north of starting area.
-  Metaglio Right: east of starting area.
-  Gold Ring: south of starting area.
-  Metaglio Left: west of starting area.
## Relics
Three relics are located in Moongolow.
-  Glass Vizard: Sold by the  Merchant
-  Emerald Disk: Sold by  Xanthia
-  Joker Disk: Also sold by Xanthia

## Trivia
- There used to be a stage with a placeholder name "Old Sanctuary", which is a reference to the song "The Sinking Old Sanctuary", which is the theme song of the Atlantis Shrine in Castlevania: Bloodlines. While the placeholder name has since been revised, Moongolow is internally referred to in the code as "SINKING".
- This stage is likely named after Moonglow, a city from the role-playing game series Ultima. The spell to unlock it ("honesty") references the virtue Moonglow was founded on in that series.
- Before version v1.0, the stage's description was as follows: "When the moon is red and bright, the remnants of a town swallowed by the sea come back to the surface, bringing mysteries and riches."
- This stage's thumbnail shows no enemies. Though,  Big Trouser makes an appearance in it.

Mt. Moonspell is the seventh and the final stage of the adventure "Legacy of the Moonspell". This stage is unlocked by surviving 10 minutes in the preceding stage, Tanuki Forest.
This stage shares similarities with its non-adventure counterpart, Mt.Moonspell, such as the overall design and the enemies that appear in this stage. However, the differences lie in some item spawns and the sequence of waves of enemies.

Mt.Moonspell is the first and only stage in the Legacy of the Moonspell DLC for Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked upon owning the DLC.
## Description
Ancestral home of a fallen clan, now overrun by yokai and oni. In this world where the sun doesn't shine, the night is victorious.
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this stage can be unlocked by defeating Orochimario (25:00) in normal mode.
Hyper mode in Mt.Moonspell has a +100% Gold and +10%  Luck bonus.
## Items
Mt.Moonspell map with full item spawn locations.
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
A Goshadokuro will appear when getting close of any one of following stage items.
-  Spinach: 10%, 10% chance for more Spinach to spawn. Southeast of the starting area in the pink field.
-  Pummarola: 10%, 10% chance for more Pummarola to spawn. Southwest of the starting area in the large building.
-  Candelabrador: 10%, 10% chance for more Candelabrador to spawn. North of the starting area inside the mountain cave.
-  Duplicator: north of the starting area at the end of the left path inside the mountain cave.
-  Attractorb: northwest of the starting area in the middle of the frozen lake.
-  Stone Mask: northeast of the starting area in the upper left of the walled off temple area.
-  Night Sword: north of the starting area at the end of outside mountain path.
The 4 hidden stage items are also present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.
-  Silver Ring: northwest of the starting area in a small cave.
-  Metaglio Right: east and slightly south of starting area.
-  Gold Ring: southeast of starting area.
-  Metaglio Left: west and slightly south of starting area.
## Pickups
- 14x  Little Clover:
- Up to 12 spawn northwest of the starting area in the field west of the end of the path leading to the Attractorb.
- One southeast of the starting area in the hidden forest.
- One southwest of the starting area in the zen garden.
- 1x  Gilded Clover: northeast of the starting area, directly south of the Stone Mask inside a large building,
-  Rosary: northwest of the starting area, directly north of the Attractorb.
-  Nduja Fritta Tanto: south of the starting area in the bottom middle house.
-  Orologion: southeast of the starting area in the bottom rightmost house.
- 3x  Vacuum:
- Northwest of the starting area to the left of the mountain.
- Northeast of the starting area to the right of the mountain.
- Southeast of the starting area in the hidden forest along with a Floor Chicken and Vacuum.
-  Floor Chicken: southeast of the starting area in the hidden forest.
## Relics
One relic is located in Mt.Moonspell.
-  Mt.Moonspell Map: northeast of starting area.
## Coffin
The  coffin of  Miang Moonspell is located in Mt.Moonspell. It is in the middle of the far northeast large temple next to the Stone Mask.
## Waves
NOTE: As official sources name only a few of the enemies, unit names are mostly made up based on their internal IDs and are subject to change.

## Location specific enemies
Different parts of the stage have different enemies.
| Enemies | Spawn location
| Hitotsume-kozo | Located around the village south of the starting area.
| Tanuki | Located near the shrines where the Silver Ring in the north, Metaglio Right in the east, Gold Ring in the south, and Metaglio Left in the west spawn.
| Kappa | Located at the frozen lake in the northwest of the map near the Attractorb and the river running along the south of the map near the Gold Ring.
| [Big Oni][Big Oni-2]Big Oni | Located north of the starting area in the mountain cave.
| Moonspell Flower Wall 2 | Located northwest of the starting area where the cluster of Little Clovers spawn. They are west of the end of the road leading to the frozen lake and Attracorb.
## Trivia
- The cave being full of Big Oni could be a reference to Final Fantasy 1's Earth Cave, where the left-most branch from the entrance contains encounters with mostly Ogre on every step-tile.

Neo City Chaos is the third stage of the adventure "Operation Guns". This stage is unlocked by having survived at least 15 minutes in the preceding stage, Bullet Biters.
To unlock the next stage, Operation: Gun City, the player has to kill the M78 after the 10 minute mark.
## Description
Red Falcon's betrayer bots are trashing Neo City and breaching the defenses of Corpo Inc.'s head office. We don't know what they're after, but it looks like kidnapping the president's cousin's nephew was just a distraction. Crash into downtown and start blasting, Contra Unit.

Neo Galuga is the first stage in the Operation Guns DLC for Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked upon owning the DLC.
## Description
Join Contra Force to run and gun across the relentlessly horizontal width of Galuga, from the jungle to Neo City and through the horrors lurking beyond. Let's survive determinedly!
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this stage can be unlocked by defeating Taka (25:00) in normal mode.
Hyper mode in Neo Galuga has a +25% Gold .
## Items
Neo Galuga map with full item spawn locations.
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
-  Arcana Treasure Chest
- 3x  Arma Dio
-  Homing Miss
-  Metal Claw
- 4x  Weapon Power-Ups
-  Prism Lass
-  Wings
The 4 hidden stage items are also present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.
The four of them are in the far east part of the map.
-  Silver Ring
-  Metaglio Right
-  Gold Ring
-  Metaglio Left
## Pickups
- 4x  Vacuums
- 7x  Grenade
- 6x  Barriers
- 4x  Rapid Fire
- 1x  Little Clover
(the number of pickups don't seem to vary but they switch position at every game)
## Relics
One relic is located in Neo Galuga.
-  Neo Galuga Map: located on the middle of the third bridge to the east of the starting area right before entering the city section in the middle of the map.
## Coffin
The  coffin of  Bill Rizer is located in Neo Galuga. It is in the far east of the map directly below the Arcana treasure chest.

“
A beautiful but malicious steed with all the strengths of its two compositing forms and none of the weaknesses. The size, strength, and stamina of a horse paired with the unrivaled cruelty of a trout. Those who dare mount it are swiftly dragged to the bottom of the lake, seemingly unaware that they could just get off and swim away.
— Ars Gouda, p. Fs 17
Njuggles is an enemy that appears in Lake Foscari.
Njuggles takes reduced knockback.

The map of Ode to Castlevania as it initially appears.
Ode to Castlevania is a stage in the eponymous DLC of Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked upon owning the DLC.
## Description
As myriad humans from all over the world joined their vespers in unison, the forces of chaos resonated and granted them their dark wish. The King of Terror raises yet again, and with him his demon castle: Castlevania.
## Overview
Ode to Castlevania is a massive map with 9 separate biomes, each with their own characters and enemy spawns, and 30 spawnable bosses.
List of biomes:
- Forest
- Gallery
- Alchemy
- Library
- Tower
- Aqueduct
- Underground
- Chapel
- Entrance
Moving between biomes keeps the wave of biome that the player was in when the minute started, with the exception of special enemy spawns in unique locations.
## Hyper mode
The hyper mode for this stage can be unlocked by defeating Malphas (25:00) in normal mode.
Hyper mode in Ode to Castlevania has a +90% Gold and +10% Luck bonus.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
-  Sinestro Custos
-  Arma Dio (×4)
-  Sonic Dash
-  Dextro Custos
-  Dominus Anger
-  Curved Knife
-  Endo Gears
-  Tirajisú (×2)
-  Epi Head
-  Shuriken
-  Dominus Hatred
-  Parm Aegis
-  Peri Pendulum
-  Karoma's Mana
-  Myo Lift
-  Familiar Forge
-  Discus
The following weapons can be picked up from defeating stage bosses.
-  Rock Riot
-  Ice Fang
-  Gale Force
-  Fulgur
-  Keremet Bubbles
-  Hex
-  Refectio
-  Bone
The 4 hidden stage items are also present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.
-  Silver Ring: north of starting area.
-  Metaglio Right: east of starting area.
-  Gold Ring: south of starting area.
-  Metaglio Left: west of starting area.
## Pickups
-  Wall Chicken
-  Karma Coin
-  Heart Refresh
-  Mirror of Truth
-  Neutron Bomb
## Relics
Five relics can be found in Ode to Castlevania.
-  Castlevania Map
-  Stallion Gate
-  Scorpion Gate
-  Capra Gate
-  King's Gate
The 6 hidden relics can be obtained if certain requirements have been met.
-  Black Disk
-  Serpent Gate
-  Beast Gate
-  Pile of Secrets
-  Wood Carving Score
-  Ebony and Crimson Stones
## Coffins
The  Coffin of  Leon Belmont is located in Ode to Castlevania, found near the Tirajisú at the drawbridge of the castle.
The coffin of  Julius Belmont is located in the far northeast of the starting area.
The coffin of  Vlad Tepes Dracula can be found in the Throne Room when entered with Richter Belmont.
The coffin of  Reinhardt Schneider can be found in the lower section of the castle, which is accessible after unlocking Dracula.

## Traps
Stepping on one of the pentagrams scattered around map on the ground triggers an unavoidable map event, spawning a predetermined boss which is guaranteed to drop a treasure chest.

## Trivia
- The Cathedral (the top part of the far east building) has spacious prayer room located in the bottom-left area of the Cathedral with a pseudo-high/standing cross in the middle with a caped, praying statue at the base. This is practically a replica to the Stage 1 intro of Castlevania III where Trevor is seen kneeling in front of a cross before embarking on his quest.

Old Mad Forest is the first stage of the adventure "A Garlic Paradise". It is unlocked at the start of that adventure.
If the player survives at least 10 minutes in this stage, they will be able to unlock the second stage, Rough Awakening.
## Description
Poe recalls a simpler time, when surviving the madness of the forest and hoarding masses of garlic was all that mattered.
## Tips
This stage can be quite difficult, as  Poe Ratcho has very limited unlocks available.  Cooldown-centric builds, utilizing Survarots,  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX), and  Empty Tome alongside a source of invulnerability such as  Laurel can provide a significant boost to survivability, potentially making one completely invulnerable, while also strengthening the rest of your build.

| Regular | Hyper
| Chapter 1[Attack Aggressively]Attack AggressivelyAttack AggressivelyContra Unit, your new orders are in. The president's son has been kidnapped by mercenaries, and they're demanding advanced military technology from Corpo Inc. as ransom - but we don't negotiate with terrorists. You're the only team though enough to take them on. Airdrop into Galuga Jungle, find their base, and blow it the heck up! | Unlocked by defaultMODIFIERS 20:00 Time Limit +25% Move Speed +100% Luck | Unlocked by defaultMODIFIERS 20:00 Time Limit +90% Move Speed +50% Gold +120% Luck | Defeat the Metal Alien after 10 minutes.
| Chapter 2[Bullet Biters]Bullet BitersBullet BitersRats! These are no mere mercenaries, they're Red Falcon fighters, traitors of the Alien Wars. We thought that group was destroyed, but it seems like they're back from the dead to kidnap the president's nephew - and they've got you surrounded. Survive until we can send a rescue chopper! | Unlocked by finding and destroying the Metal Alien in Attack Agressively[sic]MODIFIERS 30:00 Time Limit +25% Move Speed +100% Luck | Unlocked by finding and destroying the Metal Alien in Attack Agressively[sic]MODIFIERS 30:00 Time Limit +90% Move Speed +50% Gold +120% Luck | Survive for 15 minutes
| Chapter 3[Neo City Chaos]Neo City ChaosNeo City ChaosRed Falcon's betrayer bots are trashing Neo City and breaching the defenses of Corpo Inc.'s head office. We don't know what they're after, but it looks like kidnapping the president's cousin's nephew was just a distraction. Crash into downtown and start blasting, Contra Unit. | Unlocked by surviving for 15 minutes in Bullet BitersMODIFIERS 20:00 Time Limit +25% Move Speed +100% Luck | Unlocked by surviving for 15 minutes in Bullet BitersMODIFIERS 20:00 Time Limit +90% Move Speed +50% Gold +120% Luck | Defeat the M78 after 10 minutes.
| Chapter 4[Operation: Gun City]Operation: Gun CityOperation: Gun CityMultiple asteroid impacts have been detected in Neo City and are interfering with our comms. Red Falcon have combined Alien Cells with advanced Corpo Inc. beacon technology and have summoned an invasion force! | Chapter 5[Run & Gun]Run & GunRun & GunCome in, Contra Unit, this is Headquarters. We can't get a read on your position and we aren't exactly sure what's going on without accurate reconnaissance - just keep gunning down aliens and running to the right until we say otherwise. | Unlocked by defeating Big Bot Gordea after 10 minutes in Operation: Gun CityMODIFIERS 30:00 Time Limit +25% Move Speed +100% Luck | Unlocked by defeating Big Bot Gordea after 10 minutes in Operation: Gun CityMODIFIERS 30:00 Time Limit +90% Move Speed +50% Gold +120% Luck | Find a new ally

You're our only hope, the only squad strong enough to survive in these stinking egg-spawning grounds - shoot those evil space creatures and win the war for humanity. | Unlocked by finding a new ally in Run & GunMODIFIERS 30:00 Time Limit +25% Move Speed +100% Luck | Unlocked by finding a new ally in Run & GunMODIFIERS 30:00 Time Limit +90% Move Speed +50% Gold +120% Luck | Defeat Taka after 25 minutes

Operation: Gun City is the fourth stage of the adventure "Operation Guns". This stage is unlocked once the player has defeated the M78 after the 10 minute mark in the preceding stage, Neo City Chaos.
To unlock the next stage, Run & Gun, the player has to defeat the Big Bot Gordea after the 10 minute mark.
## Description
Multiple asteroid impacts have been detected in Neo City and are interfering with our comms. Red Falcon have combined Alien Cells with advanced Corpo Inc. beacon technology and have summoned an invasion force! Civilians have been evacuated, so destroy anything that moves, and see if you can find the president's cousin's nephew's dog.

Pile Assault is a map event that appears in Dairy Plant, Gallo Tower, and Laborratory. It summons a ring of enemies that surrounds the player and attacks from a distance, shooting bullets at regular intervals.
## Appearances
NOTE: Range determines the distance where enemies will be spawned relative to the player's position. Higher range values spawn enemies farther from the player, while lower range values spawn them closer.

Planar Bazaar is a unique shop stage that is exclusively available in all adventures. It is usually unlocked by reaching level 80 for the first time in any stage of an adventure; the only exception to this is in the Operation Guns adventure, which requires reaching level 80 in Alien Revengeance.
The purpose of Planar Bazaar is to be an area for the player to purchase weapons and passive items, making them permanently available in the adventure that they are playing in. The player can only purchase a maximum of six weapons or passive items at a time before the merchants become unable to sell anything. However, the player can simply exit and reenter Planar Bazaar to make more purchases.
## Description
Coiling betwixt the seconds of forgotten times, allies from the past and future gather in this confusing commercial space to trade items from other realms.
## List of merchants and available items
In Planar Bazaar, there are many characters who serve as merchants, each of which offer their set of weapons or passives for a set price.
The following is a list of characters with the available items that they sell.

## Trivia
- This is the only stage in the game to have x0 clock speed, which means that the clock remains static at 00:00.
- That also means that this is the only stage the player could never die in, as there are also zero enemies. However, since the release of Ode to Castlevania,  Dominus Anger,  Dominus Hatred and  Dominus Agony cause self-inflicting damage to the player and can cause the character to die if any of these weapons are purchased on stage.
- Hyper mode of this stage is not accessible through normal means.
- This is one of few times that in-game characters themselves have speaking roles.
- At one point, all of the items that  Sir Ambrojoe sold only cost 1.
- In base game, all of the characters of the Belpaese family, except  Zi'Assunta Belpaese, offer only passives. Meanwhile, the rest of the characters offer weapons, except for  Avatar Infernas, who offers  Torrona's Box, a passive, and two weapons, the  Flames of Misspell and  Greatest Jubilee.
- The most expensive weapon offered is the  Victory Sword, costing 10,800.
-  Santa Ladonna is the character that sells the most items among base game characters, at 8 items being offered by her.
-  Horse,  Master Librarian and  Mr. S are the characters that sells the most items across all DLCs, at 16 items each.
-  Avatar Infernas is the only secret base game character that sells items in Planar Bazaar.
- He is joined by  Master Librarian and  Hammer from the Ode to Castlevania DLC.
- In versions prior to 1.14.107, Ante Chamber is the only DLC that does not feature a character or items to offer.
- The theme of this stage is Dust Elementals, which is also the track that is used for the Bestiary.
-  Xanthia is the only character in this stage that is not a playable character.
- They are also the only character shown in the stage thumbnail.

[Map of Polus Replica]Map of Polus Replica
Polus Replica is the first and only stage in the Emergency Meeting DLC for Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked upon owning the Emergency Meeting DLC.
## Description
Summoned to a mysterious science station amongst the stars, survival is far from certain. Extraterrestrial evil and inter-dimensional duplicity lurk behind every space rock.
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this stage can be unlocked by defeating Suspicious Eyes (25:00) in normal mode.
Hyper mode in Polus Replica has a +100% Gold and +10% Luck bonus.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
-  Lifesign Scan: east of the starting area in the northeast of the map in the MedBay room.
-  Mini Crewmate: south of the starting area in the center of the map in the Office room.
-  Mini Engineer: south of the starting area, west of the center of the map in the Weapons room.
-  Mini Ghost: southwest of the starting area in the west part of the map in the O2 room.
-  Mini Shapeshifter: east of the starting area, outside of the Laboratory and Medbay rooms.
-  Mini Guardian: south of the starting area in the Communications room.
-  Mini Impostor: far southeast of the starting area in the ruins.
-  Mini Scientist: southeast of the starting area in the Specimen Room.
-  Treasure Chest: far south of the starting area, below the lava river.
The 4 hidden stage items are also present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.
-  Silver Ring: far west of the starting area.
-  Metaglio Right: far east of the starting area.
-  Gold Ring: far south of the starting area.
-  Metaglio Left: far southwest of the starting area.
## Pickups
- 5x  Vacuums are found in the west, east, center, far south, and far southeast of the stage.
-  Nduja Fritta Tanto: located in the far southwest, along the lava river.
## Relics
One relic is located in Polus Replica.
-  Polus Replica Map: Located south of the Dropship starting area in the center of the map in the Admin room
## Coffin
The  coffin of  Crewmate Dino is located in Polus Replica. It is in the south part of the map just below the snow and east of the lava rivers.

## Tips
- The chests that drop from preventing sabotages are always guaranteed to give 5 items. Opening one while all the player's weapons do no require further level ups can guarantee a  Candybox.
## Trivia
- Polus Replica is a larger replica of the Polus map in the video game Among Us. Hence the name.

The relentless pursuit of immortality of its original founders, through atrocious rituals, spawned awful abominations.","version3":"Inverse","dmax":"","update":"Legacy of the Bloodmoon is out now!","dlc":"bm","enemy-speed per min":"","image":"","time":"30:00","enemy-health":"x1","xp":"x1","enemy-speed":"","theme":"","dchance":"","luck":"x1","type":"Special","greed":"x1.5","adventure":"No","id":"BMN_BLOODMOON","starting-spawns":"","player-speed":"x1.4","version1":"Normal","projectile-speed":"","enemy-health per min":"","name":"Red Moon Manor","dchance-max":"","release":"28 August 2026","version2":"Hyper","enemy-min":"","effects":""}
Red Moon Manor is a stage in the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked upon owning the DLC.
## Description
The mythical place sought by the vile Bloodmoon Clan, said to hide terrible secrets. The relentless pursuit of immortality of its original founders, through atrocious rituals, spawned awful abominations.
## Overview
Red Moon Manor is the XL stage of Legacy of the Bloodmoon. It features 4 unique bosses that can be summoned by entering specific areas of the stage or by pressing plates that summon them:
-  Ashtart: Appears whenever the player enters the manor itself.
-  Sargon: Summoned by a button on the north side of the stage, located above  Descent Into Misery
-  Congregation: Appears whenever the player enters the maze section of the stage.
-  Baal'Thasar: Located at the opposite end of the Room 1665-like area, which is accessed by the pentagram-teleporter in the center of the maze.
- Defeating Baal'Thasar will end the stage via the  White Hand.
Whenever a boss is summoned, the edges of the screen will briefly flash red, indicating their presence. If a boss ends up off-screen, then their location will be followed with a red guide arrow.
Power Creep options, being  Golden Eggs and  Survarots, will be disabled whenever the player enters the stage until they have defeated all 4 main bosses. Defeating all 4 bosses in the same run will unlock  Nameless Saint as a playable character.
In the center of the maze, there is a pentagram that will teleport the player to an area that is nearly identical to Room 1665. On the side of this area where the player spawns, there is a pentagram to transport them back to the stage. To progress in this area, the player must defeat the bosses that can be summoned by buttons in the middle of certain rooms. At the opposite end of this section exists a second teleporter that will transport the player to Baal'Thasar's fight. 
- Sources of flight and wall clipping will not allow the player to bypass these walls; bosses must be defeated to progress.
- Each player in co-op mode must teleport to this area, and the following fight, on their own.
Approaching certain stage items, and being in specific areas of the stage, will summon  Jaman Jatos. Defeating 6000 Jaman Jatos will unlock  Jaman Jato ★ as a playable character.
- The recommended location for defeating many Jaman Jatos is the alcove in the northeast section of the maze, above the northmost  Pummarola.
## Hyper mode
The hyper mode for this stage can be unlocked by defeating  Batti Lei, which spawns at 25:00. 
It can also be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "midadeltu".
Hyper mode in Red Moon Manor has a +60%  Move Speed bonus, a +50% Gold bonus, and a +10%  Luck bonus.
## Items
## Stage items
The following items can be found in this stage:
-  Attractorb (x2)
-  Descent Into Misery
-  Candelabrador (x2)
-  Pummarola (x2)
-  Duplicator (x2)
-  Torrona's Box (x2)
-  Velvet Dodecahedron
- The player must collect this passive once before it can appear
-  Arma Dio
-  108 Responsive Prayers
- The player must collect this weapon once before it can appear
-  Arcana Treasure Chest: located on the far west side of the stage
The 4 hidden stage items are also present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.
-  Silver Ring: northwest of  Sargon
-  Metaglio Right: northeast of  Ashtart
-  Gold Ring: southwest of the  In' Kujata statue
-  Metaglio Left: far west side of the stage, to the north of the Arcana chest
If the player owns Legacy of the Moonspell and has collected the following items, they will also appear on the stage:
-  108 Bocce: located to the northwest of spawn
-  Pearl magatama: located in the northwest corner of the stage on the unmapped frozen lake
## Pickups
-  Rosary (x3)
-  Vacuum (x3)
-  Floor Chicken (x3)
-  Orologion (x1)
-  Nduja Fritta Tanto (x2)
-  Little Clover (x3)
## Relics
One relic can be found in this stage:
-  Red Moon Manor Map: located to the northeast of spawn, past the first doorway
## Coffin
The  coffin of  Malice Bloodmoon is located in Red Moon Manor. It is to the southwest of Descent Into Misery, before the entrance to the maze section of the map.
## Waves
TBA

Retirement is the sixth and the final stage of the adventure "A Garlic Paradise". This stage is unlocked by reaching at least level 50 in Garlic Paradise, the preceding stage.
## Description
At last, Poe can retire, and simply stand still forever as he has always dreamed. But will a perfectly sedentary lifestyle be enough for our malodorous hero?
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
The 4 hidden stage items are present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign.
-  Silver Ring: 7 tilesets, north of the starting area.
-  Metaglio Right: 7 tilesets, east of the starting area.
-  Gold Ring: 7 tilesets, south of the starting area.
-  Metaglio Left: 7 tilesets, west of the starting area.

(Special)","luck":"x1","adventure":"No","id":"DEVILROOM","starting-spawns":"0","player-speed":"x1.9","version1":"Normal","projectile-speed":"x1.15","version3":"Inverse","name":"Room 1665","greed":"x1.5","release":"15 August 2024","dchance-max":"80%","enemy-min":"20","greed2":"x1.5"}
Room 1665 is a hidden stage in Vampire Survivors, unlocked by using the spell "forbiddenbox". As opposed to other spells which typically bypass unlock requirements, using this spell is both the only way to unlock this stage and the intended method, as reflected in the description of the stage's achievement, which explicitly tells players to Dare to cast the spell "forbiddenbox". It contains the  Darkasso relic.
## Description
Seemingly bottomless, the down-reaching doors of this huge, infernal contraption open only when fed the blood of monsters. Summoned by the baneful behavior of a long forgotten clan, the unfathomable depths of these haunted halls whisper with a putrid power that yearns for innocent souls to play with.
## Effects
Upon starting the stage, all modifiers except  Arcanas and  Limit Break are disabled.  Curse,  Charm, and Power Creep options are also disabled.
The player starts in a small locked room with limited space to move. Defeat enemies to unlock additional rooms to the south.
There are a total of 16 rooms (including the player's spawn room) connected by corridors. Rooms are unlocked based on the total number of enemies defeated. Each room unlocks at specific kill milestones: 100, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600, 700, 800, 900, 1000, 1100, 1200, 1300, 1400, and finally 1665.
The type of enemies encountered may change depending on how many rooms have been unlocked and how much time has passed. Boss enemies generally have less health compared to non-boss enemies.
Unlike in normal stages, enemies do not despawn when they are outrun or move out of the player’s view. Additionally, there is no option to select an Arcana when the player first spawns. Instead, the player must defeat 100 enemies to unlock the next room, which contains the arcana chest.
There are many light sources placed at predictable locations. Around 11-12 light sources are typically found in each corridor connecting the rooms, and they may slowly respawn over time.
The  Darkasso is located in the final room. The stage becomes progressively darker with each new room unlocked. However, when the final room is opened, the entire stage lights up again. There is little point in continuing after obtaining 1665 kills and acquiring the Darkasso Relic.
## Trivia
- Small, eerie visual details in this stage:
- Blood drips down from the top of the screen after enough kills
- In addition, the following visual details below may regularly appear for split seconds:
- A large eye on the walls
- Shadows of arms reaching up from the bottom of the screen
- An open mouth with sharp teeth, and a pair of eyes, in the empty space outside the room

Killing 6,000 Rotting Ghouls is the requirement for unlocking Rottin'Ghoul. This count does not include the unlisted variants of Rotting Ghoul.
Rotting Ghoul takes reduced knockback.

← Sam the Sundown ClownSammy the CaterpillarBrownie →
“
It’s the caterpillar cake from Vampire Survivors(™) and the Vampire Survivors family of products! If you’re aged 30+, you owe it to yourself to have Sammy the Caterpillar for your birthday cake.Sammy the Caterpillar is a trademark of the Vampire Survivors(™) brand. Sammy the Caterpillar is not suitable for children of ages 1-29. Sammy the Caterpillar is not edible. Sammy the Caterpillar is not responsible for any Sammy the Caterpillar related injuries.
— Ars Gouda, p. Fs 10
Sammy the Caterpillar is an enemy that appears in Lake Foscari. It moves horizontally across the screen instead of chasing the character. It becomes the only enemy spawned when entering certain areas in Lake Foscari.
When defeated, it drops a  birthday cake.
Killing 6,000 Sammy the Caterpillars is the requirement for unlocking  Sammy.
## Variants
## Sammy the Caterpillar

## Appearances
Sammy the Caterpillar
| Stage | Minute | Spawn Type | Notes
| Lake Foscari | 0:00.1 | Event | Spawn 1, last 60 seconds.

| Lake Foscari | 7:00 | Event | Spawn 1, last 30 seconds, 50% chance, 5 times.
| Lake Foscari | - | Special | To the west of the starting area near the Little Clover patch; or north-east of the starting area, beyond the forest maze with a secret entrance from the top that uncovers the area.
Special Sammies areas on map
## Trivia
- Sammy the Caterpillar is likely a reference to Colin the Caterpillar, a chocolate roll cake produced by British retailer Marks & Spencer. The description of this enemy is possibly referring to a recent controversy regarding many other supermarkets copying the style of this cake and making their own caterpillar cakes, with various names beginning in 'C'. M&S took legal action against one company, Aldi, and settled for an undisclosed amount.
- The enemy's ID, FS_CAKE, makes a reference to this cake.

# Shooting Star
| Shooting Star
| 
| Map event
| Release | ? (edit)
| ID | SHOOTING_STAR
| Type | Event
| Appears in | Gallo Tower, Tiny Bridge
| {"sprite":"","type":"Event","description":"","id":"SHOOTING_STAR","notes":"","stages":"Gallo Tower, Tiny Bridge","name":"Shooting Star"}
Shooting Star is a map event that appears in Gallo Tower and Tiny Bridge. It rains groups of falling stars towards the player. The stars impact 2 seconds after an indicator marks their location, dealing 30 damage in the area.
## Mechanics
The landing locations are chosen at random within an area around of the player, with maximum distance determined by 'range'. 'Amount' specifies how many stars fall in each repeat and 'interval' the time between each falling star in the repeat.

Space 54 is the fourth Bonus Stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by collecting a total of 5  Gold Fingers across any number of runs.
## Description
The 54th ritual is complete, and space itself has folded under the weight of an otherworldly will. This veil between cosmic planes is dimensionally misaligned, phasing in unlikely friends, nightmarish foes, and a curio of immense chaotic potential.
## Effects
TBA
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this stage is unlocked by default after unlocking the stage.
Hyper mode in Space 54 has +80% Gold, +90% Move Speed and +10% Luck bonuses.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
-  Candelabrador
-  Empty Tome
-  Phas3r
## Relics
One relic is located in Space 54.
-  Brave Story: appears north or south of the player at 18:00. This will appear directly South of the Western  Gold Finger or to the Southwest of where the merchants appear on the map.

## Tips
- As Menya Moonspell, the player can travel North and find a platform to the left. Once on the platform, enter her boost state and hug the bottom left of the platform. If done correctly, the player will clip through the platform and can move freely.
## Trivia
- Space 54, along with its accompanying character  Space Dude and the  Phas3r weapon, are all references to Phaser, the game engine that Vampire Survivors was built on.
- The name Space 54 might be a reference to Studio 54, a Broadway theater that was formerly a famous nightclub during the late 20th century. In its heyday, the venue was known for its exclusivity, extravagant events, bright lights, and for popularizing disco music as a genre. The Phas3r fires projectiles accompanied by the backing track of "I Feel Love" by Donna Summer, a famous disco song whose riff was composed by Italian composer Giorgio Moroder. While not being explicitly space themed, its iconography did feature a "moon and spoon" logo.

Space Rocks is the fifth stage of the adventure "Emergency Meeting". To unlock this stage, the player has to find the  Ghost Lino in the preceding stage, Don't Get Ghosted.
To unlock the next stage, Emergency Meating, the player has to find the  Scientist Mina in this stage.
## Description
Something suspicious is going on back at the base. Angry aliens are attacking, Crewmates are missing, and all sort of paranormal creatures (though definitely no ghosts) lurk in every corner. Let's figure out what happened using the power of logic and science!

# Stages
Dungeons
Mad Forest is the first stage available to the player.
Stages are the playing fields where the player fights off hordes of enemies to survive as long as possible until the stage's time limit is reached.
The game currently features 80 official stages, divided into 33 primary stages and 47 adventure mode-exclusive stages. The primary stages include 20 stages from the base game, consisting of 5 normal stages, 7 bonus stages, 9 challenge stages, 2 special stages, and 1 hidden stage, as well as 9 additional stages from the DLCs. Adventure mode, on the other hand, includes 13 stages from the base game and 34 stages from all DLC adventures combined (not counting the identical repeating stages of the main game). Stages are unlocked by completing specific requirements tied to their corresponding achievements, without the need to be bought with coins. The exception is Mad Forest, which is the first playable stage and is unlocked by default.
## Overview
Stages are the primary areas where players fight against enemy waves, with the goal of surviving until the time limit ends, which is at 30:00, 20:00, or 15:00 depending on the stage.
Stages differ in their layout and structure. Some, such as Mad Forest, Green Acres, and The Bone Zone, feature open areas that allow players to move freely. Others, like Inlaid Library, Gallo Tower, and Tiny Bridge, have more confined designs, restricting movement to specific directions. Stages also vary in their environmental features, with some containing obstacles, walls, or corridors that require players to navigate carefully. Certain stages include interactive elements, such as trap pizzas, which are pressure plates that trigger map events when activated, or the teleporting doors in Astral Stair, which lead to rooms accessible only through a specific door.
Players can interact with various objects found in stages. Destructibles are common and can be broken by hitting them with the player’s weapons. The most frequently encountered destructible is the light source, which appears in all stages and drops a random pickup item when destroyed. Some stages also contain unique destructibles, such as the Dairy Carts in Dairy Plant or the levers in Laborratory. Additionally, stage items are pre-placed objects that players can collect, including weapons, passive items, pickups, and relics. Stages containing an uncollected relic will have an exclamation mark indicated to the left of the stage thumbnail in the stage selection screen. Other notable features are the coffins, which are found in certain stages and can be opened to unlock new characters, and the Merchant, which allows the player to purchase rare weapons, power-ups, Golden Eggs, and more.
## Completing a stage
Example of a "Stage Completed" screen in Moongolow.
If the player survives until the stage's time limit, the stage is considered complete. Upon the time limit, one  Reaper will arrive every minute to slay the player and end their run, unless Endless mode is enabled. In non-endless mode, the player can take a step further by using the  Infinite Corridor and/or the  Crimson Shroud to kill the Reaper and trigger the  White Hand event, though it is not necessary to complete a stage.
After completing the stage, the player will earn 500 gold coins as an additional reward upon death. The amount can be further increased by an additional 100 coins for each unused revival until the end of the player's run; revivals used beyond that point will still contribute towards the bonus coins.
In the mobile version of the game, if the player did not complete a stage, they may get an option to watch an advertisement to gain one revival. Furthermore, if they get to the results screen, they may get an option to watch an advertisement to earn extra gold.
## Interface
Stage Selection menu
The Stage Selection menu becomes accessible after selecting a character in the Character Selection menu and having unlocked Inlaid Library, the second stage of the game (otherwise, a new player would immediately hop onto Mad Forest). The interface displays a list of available stages, each accompanied by its respective information. Players can also activate their unlocked modifiers here before entering a stage.
The following sections provide detailed explanations of the other features visible in the Stage Selection menu.
## Stage modifiers
[Stage modifiers window]Stage modifiers windowEach stage applies modifiers to certain player and enemy stats. Aside from the stage's base modifiers, extra modifiers can be added by enabling modes. Modifiers can affect the player's movement speed, projectile speed, gold gain, and luck, as well as the enemies' movement speed, health, and quantity. The stage's base modifiers and bonuses conferred by selected modes stack additively, from where the total value of combined bonuses is then applied as a multiplier to the stat boosts from passive items and PowerUps. The Luck bonus is an exception, as it is added to the selected character's Luck stat instead.
## Modes and gameplay modifiers
Modes and gameplay modifiers window
Modes and gameplay modifiers are mechanics that change the way stages are played by adjusting difficulty and adding new features. Modes modify the base stats of both players and enemies, often increasing the challenge of stages while providing additional rewards. Gameplay modifiers, on the other hand, introduce new mechanics or adjust existing ones, allowing players to enhance their performance or alter the dynamics of their runs.
The game includes four unlockable modes: Hyper, Hurry, Inverse, and Endless. Each mode applies specific changes to stage gameplay. Additionally, there are also four unlockable gameplay modifiers: Arcanas, Limit Break, and the Randomizers (which consist of Random Events and Random LevelUp).
Modes and gameplay modifiers can be activated before starting a stage through the Stage Selection menu. Players can enable multiple options at the same time, allowing for combinations of modes and modifiers to create varied gameplay experiences.
## List of Modes
## Hyper Mode
Hyper Mode increases the character's and enemies' movement speed by 65%–75%, the projectile speed by 15%–25%, and the gold multiplier by 50%. On some stages, hyper mode also provides bonuses to the player's luck stat and enemies' HP. The overall bonuses granted by hyper mode differ with each stage, the values of which are displayed on the Stage Modifiers window.
Unlocking a stage's Hyper Mode varies depending on the stage. Most stages require defeating a specific boss enemy to unlock the hyper mode for that stage, some challenge stages require unlocking a number of hyper modes from other stages in order to unlock their own, while some stages already have hyper mode unlocked for them.
Detailed information regarding the exact values of bonuses conferred by hyper mode for each stage and their unlock requirements is contained in this section.
## Hurry Mode
Hurry Mode doubles the stage's Clock Speed and increases XP gain by 25%. The increased Clock Speed causes the in-game timer to move twice as fast, therefore halving the time required to reach a stage's completion in real time. All time-based events (e.g., enemy spawns, evolution chest drops, wave events) occur at their usual timestamps on the in-game timer.
Hurry Mode is unlocked by collecting the  Sorceress' Tears relic, located in Gallo Tower.
## Inverse Mode
Inverse Mode increases gold gain by +200% and the Luck bonus by +20%. In addition, enemies start with +200% HP and acquire +5% HP and +0.5 movement speed per minute. This effect is additive with other enemy bonus modifiers from specific stages, such as The Bone Zone and Bat Country.
In Inverse Mode, the stage's layout is rotated by 180°, and the map becomes darker and different in hue. On certain stages, stage items may be placed differently due to the inverted layout. The layout inversion effect can be toggled in the Settings, using the "Visually Invert Stages" option in the Gameplay section.
In this mode, the merchant also offers  Skips,  Banishes,  Rerolls, and an extra  Arcana.
When playing in Inverse Mode on some stages, the stage theme will change to the alternate track if available. However, playing as a character with a unique theme (such as  Pugnala Provola) will override this effect.
Inverse Mode is unlocked by collecting the  Gracia's Mirror relic, located in Eudaimonia Machine.
## Endless Mode
Endless Mode disables the spawning of  Reapers upon reaching the stage's time limit. Instead, reaching the final minute of a stage causes the enemy waves to restart from the beginning, completing a "cycle," allowing the stage to continue indefinitely. After completing the first cycle, the stage will be considered complete. Every cycle, enemies gain +100% of their base HP, their spawn frequency and quantity increase by +50%, and their damage dealt increases by +25%; also, the player's max incoming damage cap is reduced by 1.
In this mode, the merchant respawns after each cycle and begins offering one additional Revival in place of  Golden Eggs, as well as an extra Arcana if Inverse Mode is also enabled.
Some characters, like  Megalo Menya Moonspell,  Ghost Lino,  Death and  Megalo Death, still have their run ended when the stage Time Limit is reached even with Endless Mode turned on.
Endless Mode is unlocked by collecting the  Seventh Trumpet relic, located in Eudaimonia Machine.
## List of gameplay modifiers
## Arcana
Main article: Arcanas
The Arcanas are a set of individual modifiers styled after tarot cards, each with its own set of effects and changes to certain aspects of gameplay.
With Arcanas activated, the player can obtain three of them throughout a run, the first of which is given at the start of the run and the other two from Arcana chests dropped by special bosses at specific timestamps. Although only three Arcanas can normally be possessed simultaneously, the player can have an unlimited number of Arcanas. Having more than three Arcanas is possible by playing as certain characters who can obtain additional Arcanas (such as Santa), starts with an extra Arcana (such as Avatar), playing with Inverse Mode on, or using the Moonlight Bolero (VI) Darkana.
The Arcana system is unlocked by collecting the Randomazzo relic located in Gallo Tower, along with the first Arcana for the player, Sarabande of Healing (VI). Collecting the Darkasso in Room 1665 expands the system to incorporate Darkanas, which increases the selection of available Arcana. Each Arcana and Darkana can be unlocked by completing their corresponding achievements.
## Limit Break
Main article: Limit Break
Limit Break enables equipped weapons to be upgraded beyond their usual limits, allowing weapons to become more powerful than what they normally are.
With Limit Break activated, upon maxing out all equipped weapon and passive items, the player will no longer receive Coin or Floor Chicken options. Instead, they will start to receive Limit Break upgrades, with each level-up providing an upgrade to a specific stat for one of the player's equipped weapons.
Weapons with Limit Break levels may still be evolved. However, the weapon will lose all of its additionally obtained stats and its level will start back from 1. Certain weapons are excluded from this rule such as SpellStrom [DLC] and Alucard Shield [DLC], which keeps the Limit Break stats of their pre-evolutions even after evolving.
Limit Break is unlocked by collecting the Great Gospel relic after defeating The Ender in Cappella Magna.
## Randomizers
[Randomizers window]Randomizers window
Randomizers are special gameplay options which adds a factor of unpredictability to the gameplay, as their name implies. Unlike other modifiers which is intended to directly enhance gameplay, Randomizers focus on increasing stage difficulty by incorporating an element of randomness to each run.
Currently, the game features two Randomizers: Random Events and Random LevelUp. These options can be accessed through a dedicated window in the Stage Selection screen, located alongside the windows for modes and other gameplay modifiers. Notably, this Randomizers window is not visible initially and only becomes available after unlocking at least one Randomizer.
Random Events
Main article: Random Events
Random Events, also referred to as Randomized Minute Events, introduce a spinning roulette to the bottom-right corner of the screen when activated before a run, which appears as The Directer's masks on three black concentric rings. At the 30-second mark of every minute, the roulette selects a random event, which then triggers at the start of the following minute. These events vary widely in their effects—some may be beneficial, others detrimental, and some simply add a touch of humor without significant impact.
The Random Events option is unlocked by collecting the Trisection relic, located in Astral Stair. Notably, this feature is always enabled by default in Astral Stair before acquiring the Trisection relic. Once unlocked, it can be toggled on or off like other stage modifiers.
Random LevelUp
Random LevelUp, when enabled before a run, removes the player’s ability to manually select items during level-ups. Instead, the game automatically designates a random weapon or passive item to be added or upgraded in the player’s inventory. If Limit Break is also enabled, Random LevelUp will continue to apply its effects to it as well.
The Random LevelUp option is unlocked by collecting the Brave Story relic, which appears in Space 54 at the 18-minute mark.
## Music selection
See also: Music
Music Selection is a feature that allows players to customize the background music of a stage by choosing from any unlocked music tracks in the game. Players can also apply modifiers to the music, which include Normal, Hyper, and Forsaken. The Normal modifier plays the track in its original form, while Hyper increases the speed and pitch of the music, and conversely, Forsaken slows the music down and lowers its pitch. 
The Music Selection menu is unlocked by collecting the Magic Banger relic, located in Green Acres.

← SketamariSun AtlanteanMoon Atlantean →
“
Powerful guardian of a race long thought extinct. Its mask represents the Sun, giver of life, subject of worship, and friend to ice cream sellers everywhere.
— Ars Gouda, p. 94
Sun Atlantean is an enemy that appears in all normal, bonus and challenge stages. Unlike most enemies, it does not spawn as part a wave or map event, but spawns upon approaching the  Gold Ring, which is one of the hidden stage items unlocked by obtaining the Yellow Sign.
## Behavior
Sun Atlantean is resistant to freeze, instant kill effects and debuffs, and it takes reduced knockback. It does not despawn, if it leaves the player's screen, but is instead teleported back, like bosses. When killed, Sun Atlantean drops a Golden Egg instead of experience.
## Variants

Suspect Security is the second stage of the adventure "Emergency Meeting". This stage is unlocked by evolving the  Report! with the  Mini Crewmate to get the  Emergency Meeting in the preceding stage, Meeting Called.
To unlock the succeeding stage, Ejected, the player has to find the  Engineer Gino in this stage.
## Description
The report got through to HQ, and the station has been placed on lockdown for our safety - which would be very reassuring if we weren't locked outside. Maybe if we can find the Engineer we could sneak back in through the vents.

“
They are not guardians that carry swords, but rather guardians of their swords. They jealously attack anybody who so much as looks at their mighty blades, which is practically everybody because look at the size of ‘em!
— Ars Gouda, p. 57
Sword Guardian is an enemy that appears in Dairy Plant and Boss Rash. It appears both as a normal enemy and boss.
Sword Guardian takes reduced knockback. Its boss variant's health is also based on the player's level and is resistant to freeze, instant kill effects, and debuffs.
Defeating the Sword Guardian boss that appears at 25:00 in Dairy Plant unlocks the hyper mode for that stage.

“
Rotund trickster, always seen carrying his tail over his shoulder. Hang on, I’m getting a note passed to me. Oh. Actually it’s not his tail. Probably best you look this up yourself.

Tanuki is an enemy that appears in Mt.Moonspell. They are located near the shrines where the  Silver Ring in the north,  Metaglio Right in the east,  Gold Ring in the south, and  Metaglio Left in the west spawn.
Tanuki takes reduced knockback.

Tanuki Forest is the sixth stage of the adventure "Legacy of the Moonspell". This stage is unlocked by defeating a total of 3,000 of Madd-Onnas in the preceding stage, Frozen Lake.
To unlock Mt. Moonspell, the final stage of the "Legacy of the Moonspell" adventure, the player has to survive 10 minutes in this stage.
## Description
Babi-Onna is freed from the freeze, and joins the Moonspell Clan as they journey to retake their homeland. Their path takes them through the forest, where a team of tricksy Tanuki assail them with a testing trail - is that a tail, or are you just pleased to see me?

← The MaddenerThe BlinderThe Ender →
“
A Reaper that considers itself a true visionary. It’s able to cast a veil of darkness around any hero it lays an obsidian finger upon, but every time it turns up for a fight the battlefield is already so full of particles, colors, and special effects that nobody can see a damn thing anyway. It contents itself by watching those heroes desperately hunt for floor chickens amidst the colorful chaos.
— Ars Gouda, p. 124
The Blinder is an enemy that appears in Abyss Foscari and Carlo Cart, and also has a chance of spawning in any stage if the player has  Call of a Mad Moon (XIII) equipped.
Blinder's health is based on the player's level, and it is immune to knockback, freeze, and debuffs.
## Instances
## Map event
Blinder appears in a map event that spawns a single black reaper that follows the player for 1 or 2 minutes, occasionally speeding up or slowing down.
In Abyss Foscari, Blinder has a low chance to spawn at the very start of the round and a slightly higher chance to spawn at the 8 minute mark, and almost guaranteed to spawn at the 12 minute mark.
## Other
In Carlo Cart, it is summoned by the Timed Enemy Spawn map event.
## Behavior
Upon spawning, The Blinder will follow the player around but will not actively make contact. Occasionally, it will speed up or slow down, appearing to chase the player, dealing massive damage upon contact. It will continue this behavior until it is defeated or its set duration has elapsed. This behavior is identical to that of The Stalker.
It can be defeated with Pentagram, Gorgeous Moon, Infinite Corridor, Crimson Shroud, Victory Sword, Luminaire [DLC], Rosary, or Grenade [DLC].
Defeating it with the aforementioned weapons and items will make it drop a Treasure Chest: 
| | Level: -Evolution: Yes
| [Treasure Chest 3] 6% | [Treasure Chest 2] 66% | [Treasure Chest] 100%
| Rewards: Evolution Upgrade (any) Upgrade (any) Evolution Evolution
## Patrols
The weaker variant of Blinder spawns when the player character is close to any stage items, with less health, damage, and MoveSpeed. As opposed to general behaviour, it cannot drop a Treasure Chest.
## Carlo Cart
The Blinder operates under its usual behavior in this stage, although with a significantly higher health-scaling than its other counterparts, making the only realistic way of defeating it is with insta-kill effects.

306030x1.25x1.9x1.25x1.25 (+0.05 per minute)x1.9 (+0.05 per minute)x1.25 (+0.05 per minute)x1x1.15x1x1.5x2x1.5x1x1.25x1
The Bone Zone is the second challenge stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by unlocking hyper mode for 3 normal stages (for example, Mad Forest, Inlaid Library, and Gallo Tower).
Alternatively, The Bone Zone can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "rottingpizza".
## Description
Calcified carnage awaits where the dead go to live. If we speak unholy vespers we will open the way, but should we not go home? Have we not dug too deep?
## Effects
In The Bone Zone light sources attempt to spawn twice as often, but only drop Gold Coins, Small Coin Bags, and Survarots (if playing as any character who can find them, such as  Jimbo [DLC]. 
- Note that pickups exclusive to Operation Guns characters may still drop as normal, but not those from Ode to Castlevania.
Enemy health and speed multipliers are increased every minute. Every minute, the enemies' health and speed scale by 0.3 and 0.05 respectively, without a cap. This effect is additive with Inverse Mode
Normal mode grants a 50% Gold bonus. 
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this map is unlocked by default after unlocking the stage.
Hyper mode in The Bone Zone has +100% Gold bonuses.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
The 4 hidden stage items are present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign. Milky Way Map displays the locations of these items. One of each item is guaranteed to appear.
-  Silver Ring: north of starting area.
-  Metaglio Right: east of starting area.
-  Gold Ring: south of starting area.
-  Metaglio Left: west of starting area.
## Relics
One relic is located in The Bone Zone.
-  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane: Defeat Sketamari due south of the starting area, moving back and forth horizontally, marked with a question mark.
## Waves
Enemies in The Bone Zone approach from all four sides of the player.
NOTE: As official sources name only a few of the enemies, unit names are mostly made up based on their internal IDs with some added flavor and are subject to change.

## Trivia
- Enabling hyper mode and inverse mode in this stage will allow players to achieve a gold bonus of +300%, which is the highest gold stage bonus you can get in the game. For more info, see here.
- (DLC) Mt.Moonspell with Hyper mode and Inverse mode enabled has the same bonus of +300%.
- Before version 0.10.0, the stage's theme was a detuned version of Dairy Plant's theme, labeled as "forsaken" in the stage music selection.
- This stage features both The Drowner and The Stalker.
- Before version 1.0, the stage's description was as follows: "Come over here and say your unholy vespers."
- Despite unlocking the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane in this stage, you can use the very same relic to unlock the stage.
- The term "unholy vespers" in the stage's description references the track "Unholy Vespers" from Castlevania: Order of Ecclesia. In that game, the track plays in the Skeleton Cave stage.

The Coop is a stage in Vampire Survivors, unlocked by collecting a total of 500  Floor Chicken, which also unlocks the stage's hyper mode. It contains the  Roast Chicken with a Clock in the Middle relic.
Alternatively, The Coop can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "coopwhen".
 Parm Aegis is unlocked by picking it up in The Coop as a stage item.
## Description
A farm for fear itself, the beasts reared here have turned from tasty morsels to terrifying monsters after realising the deadly power of cooperation.
## Overview
Every 60 seconds, one fence to the east opens up. After 15 minutes, fences stop opening up. The  Merchant appears at the beginning of the stage, but will run far to the east once the timer starts.
The game and the stage's enemies speed up significantly as time increases.
## Special starting event
At the beginning of the stage, enemy  Chickenfantries run through the area that should be killed to start the timer.
If the player does not kill any Chickenfantries for one minute, a special event with  Gaea will occur. The screen will gradually start to zoom in, the music stops, and the stage's background disappears and become a solid rainbow color, all before the Avatar of Gaea slowly approaches the player from the bottom of the screen. When it is close to the player, the background suddenly turns dark, Gaea disappears, and a strange chiming sound plays before its eyes briefly flash onto the screen for a moment before the run automatically ends. This counts as completing the stage and unlocks the  Gaze of Gaea. This event will only occur once.
An effective way to avoid killing Chickenfantries is to play as  Gennaro, immediately walk to the top or bottom of the stage, and wait there while making sure that his  Knives are flying outside the stage boundary. Alternatively, one can play as  Gallo or  Divano, and then press the Skip button once prompted with the level up screen. Alternatively, the player can either select the  Fleaman [DLC] and avoid picking up the  Experience Gem, or select  Big Trouser, then select any of the items from the  Candybox menu: the  Clock Lancet, the  Laurel, or the  Arma Dio (and then any passive item afterwards). This way, no damage will be dealt to incoming Chickenfantries.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
-  Parm Aegis: available after 1:00 (1 fence away).
-  Fire Wand: available after 5:00 (5 fences away).
## Pickups
-  Floor Chicken
## Relics
One relic can be found in this stage:
-  Roast Chicken with a Clock in the Middle: located at the end of the stage at 15:00.

## Trivia
- The name of this stage was intended to be a pun on the word "co-op".
- Before the player kills the first Chickenfantry, the stage's theme, "Martes de poncle", is played in slow motion. After the first Chickenfantry is killed, it will be played in normal speed. After the first minute, it will be played at a higher speed.
- The mechanic where the player must kill a  Chickenfantry before the level's timer starts serves as a possible reference to a recurring mechanic in The Legend of Zelda series, where if the player harms a Cucco for long enough, it will cause a swarm of Cuccos to fly in and attack the player.

← The StalkerThe DrownerThe Maddener →
“
A Reaper that loves to make it rain. Stories claim that it once did so for forty days and forty nights, simply to impress a guy. In battle, the Drowner is a master of wet work, preferring to dampen the spirits of its adversaries with a torrent of abuse. Don’t fall foul of the original dead pool.
— Ars Gouda, p. 122
The Drowner is an enemy that appears in Gallo Tower, Cappella Magna, The Lycaeum, The Bone Zone, Boss Rash, and Carlo Cart or as a Random Event.
Drowner's health is based on the player's level, and it is immune to knockback, freeze, and debuffs.
## Instances
## Map event
In most stages, The Drowner appears in a map event that spawns a single blue reaper that remains stationary at the bottom-left corner of the screen. It cannot directly approach the player but it summons a slowly rising flood that damages the player if touched.
In Gallo Tower, it is spawned by a special event where it is summoned by the Giant Enemy Crab boss, who appears at the 25-minute mark if the player spends more than 6 seconds below it. Defeating it causes it to drop a chest. Additionally, a non-event invulnerable Drowner is spawned at the 30-minute mark, alongside  The Reaper.
In Cappella Magna, it is guaranteed to spawn at 5:10 as a special event if the player has not yet defeated  The Ender. After defeating The Ender, it will no longer spawn in this stage.
In The Lycaeum, it is guaranteed to spawn once the player enters the vertical section of the stage, and can be defeated either through taking damage normally or by reaching the top of the stage.
In The Bone Zone, it is guaranteed to spawn at the 20-minute mark.
## Other
In Boss Rash, it spawns as a wave boss.
In Carlo Cart, it is summoned by the Timed Enemy Spawn map event and spawns as a boss at a later wave, appearing twice in the stage.
Can be spawned as a Random Event on any map.
## Behavior
Upon being spawned, The Drowner appears on the bottom-left corner and starts flooding the lower area of the map, slowly engulfing it as the water rises over time. Entering the water will cause the player to rapidly take 20 damage per game-tick. Freezing the Drowner also causes the flood to stop rising. The flood will disappear if Drowner is defeated or if its set duration expires.
The Drowner is instantly killed upon taking damage from Gorgeous Moon, Infinite Corridor, Crimson Shroud, Victory Sword, Rosary, or Grenade [DLC]. Unlike other Reaper types, it is unaffected by Pentagram. Additionally, it takes 10 times more damage from breaking Stained Glass windows in Cappella Magna. If The Drowner is defeated with any of the aforementioned weapons and items, a red ghost will appear in the bottom-right corner of the screen, accompanied by a jingle. 
Defeating it with instant-kill effects will make it drop a Treasure Chest: 
| | Level: -Evolution: Yes
| [Treasure Chest 3] 6% | [Treasure Chest 2] 66% | [Treasure Chest] 100%
| Rewards: Evolution Upgrade (any) Upgrade (any) Evolution Evolution
The chest can exceptionally evolve up to 3 weapons, provided that a higher-tier chest with enough rewards was received. 
## Cappella Magna and Boss Rash
A weaker variant of The Stalker spawns in Cappella Magna and Boss Rash, with less health and movement speed. As opposed to general behavior, it can be defeated with Pentagram and defeating it won't make it drop a Treasure Chest. However, instantly defeating it will still trigger the "red ghost" to appear.
## Carlo Cart
The Drowner does not perform its usual move of flooding the screen; instead, it can only attack by directly approaching the player. Similar to its instances from Capella Magna and Boss Rash, It cannot drop a Treasure Chest upon defeat and is vulnerable to Pentagram's effects.

The Guardian of the Lake is the second stage of the adventure "Tides of the Foscari". It is unlocked by evolving Genevieve Gruyère's Shadow Servant in The World Eater.
To unlock the succeeding stage, the player has to evolve the Prismatic Missile as Luminaire before 15:00.
## Description
Once upon a time, sensing a imminent danger, the noble witch Luminaire rushed to seal the tide of evil poised to roll over the world like an apocalyptic flood. Make haste, good witch, or we perish.
