The Lycaeum is a stage in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by collecting 50  Vacuums.
## Description
Myths are murmured of a school submerged beneath a sunlit lake. In silent depths strange fish survive, while at its peak vile evil thrives.
## Overview
The Lycaeum is a finite, underwater-themed stage. The bottom section interior of the stage contains bookshelves scattered about as obstacles. Enemies in this section only spawn from the east and west ends of the player's screen. Below the stage exists a large, open area that constantly spawns  Moon Anforas and, occasionally, [Moongolow Atlanteans] Moongolow Atlanteans. Light sources spawn alongside the walls in the form of blue braziers. There are three red buttons in the center, and both edges of the stage, that spawn a Giant Enemy Crab when pressed. Defeating this boss will cause it to drop an  Empty Tome. There is a large, vertical section in the center of the map.
Once the player enters the vertical section,  The Drowner will appear and can only be defeated with normal damage or by reaching the top of the stage. Every other floor contains a coffin that, once opened, drops either a  Treasure Chest or one of the Yellow Sign's stage items. Treasure Chests spawned this way can only be collected with  Mad Groove (VIII),  Edge of the Earth (VIII), or a  Lavatrix Machina. There are 10 coffins on the left side of this section, then followed by 5 buttons that summon  Itchiocentaurs, then followed by 10 coffins on the right side. As the player ascends this section, the music becomes more intense and the background slowly rises, revealing a ruined city on the surface.
 Big Troubler resides at the top of the stage, offering the  Green Eyes Vizard,  Survarocchi and, after the respective secret has been completed, the  Enemahs for 60,000, 120,000, and 180,000, respectively. Reaching the top of the stage also enables teleporters that allow the player to instantly reach halfway to the top, or the top, of this section.
Out of bounds, the stage is surrounded by an impenetrable, black border. Approaching the east, south, and west sides of this border causes the screen to gradually get darker as the player gets closer. Approaching the east and west sides of this border will cause all enemies to be replaced with  Sig.ra Blu.
 Golden Eggs are disabled until the player reaches the top of the stage at least once, and are activated alongside the teleports in the stage.
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this stage is unlocked by default after unlocking the stage.
Hyper mode in The Lycaeum has +50% Gold, +10%  Luck, and +100%  Move Speed bonuses.
## Items
The following items can be found on this stage:
-  Attractorb, which spawns to the west.
-  Penshin Fatcha, which spawns at the bottom of the vertical section.
-  Wings, which also spawns at the bottom of the vertical section.
-  Spellbinder, which spawns to the east.
-  Empty Tome, which is dropped by the crab bosses.
The 4 hidden stage items are also present, if the player has obtained the Yellow Sign:
-  Gold Ring, found in the 4th coffin from the bottom.
-  Metaglio Left, found in the 7th coffin from the bottom.
-  Metaglio Right, found in the 17th coffin from the bottom.
-  Silver Ring, found in the 18th coffin from the bottom.
## Relics
Three relics are located in The Lycaeum:
- The  Green Eyes Vizard
- The  Survarocchi
- The  Enemahs
All three relics are purchased from  Big Troubler.
## Waves
Waves in Lycaeum have the Mapped spawn type, meaning enemy spawns change depending on the player's location.
Note: As official sources name only a few of the enemies, unit names are mostly made up based on their internal IDs with some creative flair added and may be subject to change.

# The Stalker

| {"update":"Patch 0.3.0","stages":"Dairy Plant, Cappella Magna, The Bone Zone, Boss Rash","image":"Sprite-The Stalker.png","health":"2,550","resistances":"Freeze, Debuff, Knockback","release":"2 March 2022","skills":"HP x Level","movespeed":"150","damage":"50"}
| Stalker
| Please upload an image!

| {"notes":"","type":"Wave event, Special event","name":"Stalker","stages":"Dairy Plant, The Bone Zone, Cappella Magna, Carlo Cart","release":"2 March 2022","id":"STALKER","update":"Patch 0.3.0","description":""}
← The TricksterThe StalkerThe Drowner →
“
A Reaper that can’t take a hint. Once they set their eyes on a victim, nothing can dissuade them from an obsessive campaign of terror. Many of the Stalker’s victims die from exhaustion or simply surrender after years of pursuit. Beware should it take an interest in you. Wherever you go, it follows.
— Ars Gouda, p. 121
The Stalker is an enemy that appears in Dairy Plant, The Bone Zone, Cappella Magna, Boss Rash, and Carlo Cart or as a Random Event. 
Stalker's health is based on the player's level, and it is immune to knockback, freeze, and debuffs. 
## Instances
## Map event
In most stages, The Stalker appears in a map event that spawns a single green reaper that follows the player for 1 or 2 minutes, occasionally speeding up or slowing down.
In Dairy Plant, it has a low chance to spawn at the very start of the round and a slightly higher chance to spawn at the 8 and 12 minute mark.
In Cappella Magna, it is guaranteed to spawn at 10:10 as a special event if the player has not yet defeated The Ender. Since it is spawned by a special event instead of being a part of the wave, it cannot appear in Green Acres. After defeating The Ender, it will no longer spawn in this stage.
In The Bone Zone, it is guaranteed to spawn at the 10 minute mark.
## Other
In Boss Rash, it spawns as a wave boss.
In Carlo Cart, it is summoned by the Timed Enemy Spawn map event and spawns as a boss at a later wave, appearing twice in the stage.
Can be spawned as a Random Event on any map.
## Behavior
Upon spawning, The Stalker will follow the player around but will not actively make contact. Occasionally, it will speed up or slow down, appearing to chase the player, dealing massive damage upon contact. It will continue this behavior until it is defeated or its set duration has elapsed. 
Having a high Curse stat significantly increases the danger of The Stalker as it allows it to move very fast, making it extremely difficult to outrun or avoid. As such, it is advised for players to acquire at least one weapon that can defeat The Stalker when playing on a stage where it can spawn if having high Curse is not avoidable. 
The Stalker is instantly killed upon taking damage from Pentagram, Gorgeous Moon, Infinite Corridor, Crimson Shroud, Victory Sword, Rosary, Just Vent [DLC], Grenade [DLC], or the Dairy Carts found in Dairy Plant. Additionally, it takes 10 times more damage from breaking Stained Glass windows in Cappella Magna. Upon being defeated, it may drop a Treasure Chest depending on what was used on it:
- Pentagram and Grenade[Verify] cause no chest to drop, regardless of it being a good or bad proc.
- Gorgeous Moon, Infinite Corridor, Crimson Shroud, Victory Sword, and Rosary cause a high-quality chest to drop:
| | Level: -Evolution: Yes
| [Treasure Chest 3] 6% | [Treasure Chest 2] 66% | [Treasure Chest] 100%
| Rewards: Evolution Upgrade (any) Upgrade (any) Evolution Evolution
- Dairy Cart and Stained Glass windows[Verify] cause a lower quality chest to drop:
| | Level: -Evolution: Yes
| [Treasure Chest 3] 3% | [Treasure Chest 2] 10% | [Treasure Chest] 100%
| Rewards: Evolution Upgrade (any) Upgrade (any) Evolution Evolution
Both chests can exceptionally evolve up to 3 weapons if higher tier.
If Stalker is defeated with any of the aforementioned weapons and items, a red ghost will appear in the bottom-right corner of the screen, accompanied by a jingle.
## Cappella Magna and Boss Rash
A weaker variant of The Stalker spawns in Cappella Magna and Boss Rash, with less health and movement speed. As opposed to general behavior, it cannot drop a Treasure Chest. However, instantly defeating it will still trigger the "red ghost" to appear.
## Carlo Cart
The Stalker operates under its usual behavior in this stage; however, killing it regardless of the method used will not cause it to drop any Treasure Chests.
## Variants

Throne Room is the fifth stage of the "Ode to Castlevania" adventure in Vampire Survivors.
## Description
The Vampire hides, invisible in a hall of mirrors festooned with his most frightful followers. Solve his maddening riddle to reveal the awful truth.
## Overview
Throne Room

Tides of the Foscari is the sixth stage of the adventure "Tides of the Foscari". It is unlocked by reaching level 30 in the The Star Pupil chapter of the Tides of the Foscari adventure.
To unlock the succeeding stage, the player must survive 30 minutes.
## Description
Once upon a time, three students, one from each of the houses of Foscari, brave a journey into a forest (called Lake) stuffed with mythological creatures, all to have a jolly adventure, with absolutely nothing interrupting the whimsy.

# Timed Enemy Spawn
| Timed Enemy Spawn
| Please upload an image!
| Map event
| Release | ? (edit)
| ID | SPAWN_TIMED_ENEMY
| Type | Event, Boss
| Appears in | Carlo Cart
| Notes | Summons an enemy that chases the player for a set duration.
| {"type":"Event, Boss","stages":"Carlo Cart","id":"SPAWN_TIMED_ENEMY","notes":"* Summons an enemy that chases the player for a set duration.","name":"Timed Enemy Spawn"}
Timed Enemy Spawn is a map event that occurs in Carlo Cart. It summons an enemy at the beginning of a wave, chasing the player until defeated or its set duration expires.
Enemies summoned by Timed Enemy Spawn events follow the player around as they move, periodically speeding up to approach the player before slowing down, similar to The Stalker. Aside from their behavior, the spawned units' stats are identical to their normal counterparts.
## Appearances
| Stage | Minute | Enemy | Notes
| Carlo Cart | 2:00 | Cart Trickster | Spawn 1, lasts 1 minute.
| Carlo Cart | 8:00 | Cart Stalker | Spawn 1, lasts 1 minute.
| Carlo Cart | 9:00 | Cart Drowner | Spawn 1, lasts 1 minute.
| Carlo Cart | 10:00 | Cart Maddener | Spawn 1, lasts 1 minute.
| Carlo Cart | 11:00 | Cart Blinder | Spawn 1, lasts 1 minute.
## Trivia
- Currently, all of the enemies under Timed Enemy Spawns only include the different types of reapers and all have a set duration of 60 seconds; however, the template for this map event is formatted in such a way that allows for different enemy types or durations to be used with it. This indicates the possible reappearance of its usage on future stages, albeit with parameters differing from its current instances.

x1.25x1.9x1.25x1.25 (+0.25 per minute)x1.9 (+0.25 per minute)x1.25 (+0.25 per minute)x1x1.15x1x1.3x1.8x1.3? (edit)+1.1%? (edit)
Tiny Bridge is the sixth challenge stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by reaching level 80 in Inverse Gallo Tower.
Alternatively, Tiny Bridge can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "recyclethetower".
## Description
Two opposing factions abruptly stopped fighting over this little bridge. No doubt the source of this cessation will turn out to be harmless.
## Effects
Enemy health multiplier increases by an additional +0.2 per minute and the enemy speed multiplier by an additional +0.025 per minute.
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this stage is unlocked by default after unlocking the stage.
Hyper mode in Tiny Bridge has +80% Gold and +10% Luck bonuses.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
 Wings and  Vento Sacro can be found on the left side of the bridge, while  Attractorb and  Lightning Ring can be found on the right.
The Yellow Sign has no effect on this level.
## Relics
 Chaos Altemanna Can be found to the far left of the bridge, past the  Wings and  Vento Sacro.
## Waves
Because of this stage's incredibly narrow layout, enemies will usually approach from the left and right sides of the screen in large clusters.
NOTE: As official sources name only a few of the enemies, unit names are mostly made up based on their internal IDs and are subject to change.

## Trivia
- Tiny Bridge, Astral Stair, Space 54, and The Coop are currently the only non-DLC stages to have weapons as stage items.
- Tiny Bridge, Bat Country, and Eudaimonia Machine are currently the only stages that the Yellow Sign has no effect on.
- The player can escape the bridge with a well timed pause, while running against the north or south walls. Going far down north or south will result in the player returning to the map, but without the enemies.
- Interestingly, if you go down south enough, the text 'I AM ALIVE HERE' also appears, similarly to Gallo Tower.

← Mimic Season ThreeTri-AnchorsLV128 Golden Bat →
“
Its father was a great beast that arrived to this world within the belly of a meteor. Its mother was a fish. Each head has its own personality, core values, and political affiliation.
— Ars Gouda, p. 129
Tri-Anchors is an enemy that appears in Boss Rash and Carlo Cart. In Boss Rash, it appears both as a boss and a normal enemy. In Carlo Cart, it appears as a special enemy, guarding the coffin of the secret character found on the stage.
Tri-Anchors' health is based on the player's level. It is immune to knockback, freeze, debuffs, and defang. It also has no collision, allowing it to pass through walls and other enemies.
## Variants

## Trivia
- Tri-Anchors' name is a reference to Ashton Anchors, a character from the game Star Ocean: The Second Story notable for the two-headed dragon that are merged to his body.
- Tri-Anchors' design may be inspired by King Ghidorah from the Godzilla franchise, being an armless three-headed dragon, particularly an internet meme in which one of the heads (nicknamed "Kevin") appears to be more playful and humorous than the other heads, which have a more serious expression.

“
Powerful guardian of a race long thought extinct. Its mask represents fire and fury, the rage of the natural world, seething heat and opposition to a man named Joe.
— Ars Gouda, p. 97
Volcano Atlantean is an enemy that appears in all normal, bonus and challenge stages. Unlike most enemies, it does not spawn as part a wave or map event, but spawns upon approaching the  Metaglio Right, which is one of the hidden stage items unlocked by obtaining the Yellow Sign.
## Behavior
Volcano Atlantean is resistant to freeze, instant kill effects and debuffs, and takes reduced knockback. It does not despawn if leaving the player's screen, instead teleporting to the other side. When killed Volcano Atlantean drops a Golden Egg instead of experience.

Welcome to the Cursino, where you need not be a vampire to fear the stakes.","version3":"Inverse","dmax":"12","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","dlc":"","enemy-speed per min":"","image":"","time":"20:00","enemy-health":"","xp":"","enemy-speed":"","theme":"Killer Squiller","dchance":"7.5%","luck":"","type":"Challenge","greed":"x1.2","adventure":"No","id":"EX_WESTWOODS","starting-spawns":"","player-speed":"x1.25","version1":"Normal","projectile-speed":"","enemy-health per min":"","name":"Westwoods","dchance-max":"50%","release":"28 October 2025","version2":"Hyper","enemy-min":"","effects":""}
Westwoods is a stage in Vampire Survivors. It, and its Hyper Mode, are unlocked by finding 23  Little Clovers.
Alternatively, Westwoods can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "onebranchbandit".
Surviving 20 minutes in Westwoods unlocks  Chula-Reh as playable character.
 Karoma's Mana is unlocked by picking it up in Westwoods as stage item.
## Description
Greed's gone green. Arcane avarice is transforming these once serene woods into a ghoulish gambling den. Welcome to the Cursino, where you need not be a vampire to fear the stakes.
## Effects
The majority of Westwoods will be unavailable to the player at the start, using walls that are impermeable to Wall Clipping and Flight. In order to progress, the player must win the "Spin the Wheel!" minigame at the  Giocare Merchant.
## Spin the Wheel!
Spin the Wheel! is a minigame by  Giocare that allows for the player to buy a number of spins using  Gold Coins. When the player buys a spin, the wheel will immediately activate and make the player either gain a certain item, an event, or the ability to access a leftward part of Westwoods.
This article or section is incomplete and could do with improvement.
Reason: Discuss the detailed mechanics about how the wheel works
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
-  Magi-Stone, found in the starting area of the stage.
-  Gatti Amari, found in the second area of the stage, accessible after winning once.
-  Pentagram, found in the second area of the stage, accessible after winning once.
-  Karoma's Mana, found in the final area of the stage, accessible after winning twice.
## Relics
One Relic is located in Westwoods:
-  Masquerade, found at the opposite side of the stage, and can only accessed after winning the wheel-spin twice.
## Waves
Waves in Westwoods have the Standard spawn type, meaning enemies appear from all four directions of the player.
Note: As official sources name only a few of the enemies, unit names are mostly made up based on their internal IDs with some creative flair added and may be subject to change.

## Tips
- If the player is having trouble unlocking this stage,  Greatest Jubilee can spawn plenty of light sources, granting the player extra Little Clovers.
- Lake Foscari also has guaranteed Little Clovers, however, it is part of the Tides of the Foscari DLC.
- The spell "kamiki" spawns the player in their last played stage with 10 Little Clovers. This can be an easier, but more "cheat-y" way of unlocking the stage.
## Trivia
- Westwoods may have been named after Westwood Studios, a now-defunct video game developer best known for developing the Command & Conquer game series. Westwood Studios also developed The Legend of Kyrandia series of games, which may have inspired the character  Chula-Reh, as well as  Karoma's Mana,  Magi-Stone, and the  Kyra-Stones weapons.

The White Hand is a special event that takes place once the player has either defeated  The Reaper after damaging it with the  Infinite Corridor or  Crimson Shroud, or immediately after the player has defeated certain major stage bosses which are as follows:
-  The Ender
-  Avatar Infernas
-  Je-Ne-Viv [DLC]
-  Big Fuzz [DLC]
-  Baal'Thasar [DLC]
It may also spawn during the battle with  The Directer if the player has any Revivals.
## Behavior
When the White Hand event is triggered, the screen will start to turn red, the camera will slowly zoom in towards the player's character, and the sound of a church bell strikes twelve times. After the twelfth strike, the White Hand appears from the left side of the screen and will start to slowly approach the player. The White Hand's movement is relative to the player's view rather than the character's position on the map. This means that it is not possible to outrun the White Hand.
Once the White Hand has reached the player, the player will instantly die. Effects that grant invulnerability or intangibility do not work against the White Hand, because rather than dealing damage, a hardcoded event triggers when the White Hand reaches the player which sets their current health to 0, ignoring any form of invincibility. It is impossible to kill or stall the White Hand, and any time the player attempts to revive, the White Hand will immediately kill them.
The White Hand's behavior is identical when triggered any way except for the battle with The Directer in Eudaimonia Machine.
During the battle with The Directer, the White Hand may be dragged from the right side onto the screen by one of The Directer's hands to kill the player. This can happen multiple times. If the event triggers and the player has no Revivals, they are still killed by the White Hand, but are revived automatically with a special animation.
If the player has additional speed from the  Roast Chicken with a Clock in the Middle, its effect will be disabled when the event begins.
## Trivia
- Due to the Crimson Shroud and Infinite Corridor's abilities to kill The Reaper easily, this event was added as a way to bring the current run to a close.
- The White Hand is likely a reference to the Pokémon creepypasta of the same name.
- The twelve bell tolls before the White Hand's arrival are known as a death knell, done to signify someone's death or an impending death, which may reference either the Red Death dying itself, or the player's impending death.
- The game does not detect whether the White Hand has touched the player as it is a sprite, instead the kill event is triggered once the White Hand reaches the center of the screen.
- While the screen zooms in during each toll, pausing the screen zooms the game back out. This does not change the end result of the cutscene, however.

Whiteout is the third Bonus Stage in Vampire Survivors. This stage can be unlocked by collecting a total of 20  Orologions across any number of runs.
## Description
Arctic mirages and a slow, constant snowstorm make this great glacier a hostile environment. Nonetheless, the myths of a powerful weapon, a mysterious potion, and a cursed woman who hates hot springs, make for strong lures.
## Effects
Enemies are slow and weak to fire weapons. The [Fire damage] tag appears on affected weapons when they are seen in level ups. The list of fire weapons includes:
-  Fire Wand and  Hellfire
-  Santa Water and  La Borra
-  Valkyrie Turner, but not  Shadow Pinion
-  Flames of Misspell and  Ashes of Muspell
-  Bracelet,  Bi-Bracelet, and  Tri-Bracelet
-  Nduja Fritta Tanto
-  Heart of Fire (XIX)
-  Firearm [DLC] and  Fire-L3GS [DLC]
-  Alchemy Whip [DLC] and  Vampire Killer [DLC]
-  Raging Fire [DLC],  Salamender [DLC], and  Speculo Raging Fire [DLC]
-  Dominus Anger [DLC], but not  Power of Sire [DLC]
-  Dextro Custos [DLC] and  Trinum Custodem [DLC], but not  Centralis Custos [DLC] nor  Sinestro Custos [DLC]
-  Svarog Statue [DLC] and  Darkness Illusion [DLC]
-  Kardía Phlegeton [DLC], but not  Aura Blast [DLC]
-  Dies Irae [DLC], but not  Arrow of Goth [DLC] nor  Mannajja
-  Hydro Pump Climax [DLC], but not  Hydro Storm [DLC]
-  Punch [DLC],  Pressure Point [DLC], and  Gilded Hand [DLC]
-  Saber Machine Gun [DLC],  Hecaton Machine Gun [DLC], and  Divergence [DLC]
-  Spirit Rings [DLC] and  Rings of Calamity [DLC]
-  Infernolatro [DLC] and  NaneInferno [DLC]
-  FireFall [DLC],  FireBall [DLC],  FireWall [DLC], and  FireStall [DLC]
## Hyper Mode
The hyper mode for this stage is unlocked by default after unlocking the stage.
Hyper mode in Whiteout has +25% Gold and +100% Move Speed bonuses.
## Items
The following items can be found in this stage:
## Stage items
-  Glass Fandango
-  Skull O'Maniac
-  Spinach
-  Wings
-  Sorbetto (added in Patch 1.13)
## Relics
One relic is located in Whiteout.
-  Antidote: south of starting area.

Wiseparke takes reduced knockback.
